### Post #53: 2023-07-19 21:50

**$BABA**

Why I bought BABA.

I chose to take a long position on Alibaba ($BABA) due to several compelling factors.

Firstly, Alibaba is more than just a retailer. It operates Taobao, a domestic e-marketplace in China that can be compared to Amazon.com in U.S. 

Additionally, its global digital e-commerce platform, Aliexpress, covers regions like Latin America and Europe.

Alibaba's Cloud Intelligence Unit holds a significant market share (36%) in China's cloud industry, (AWS). 

Its Local Services Group, which includes food services, contributes 5% of Alibaba's total revenue and (Equivalent to Amazon Go or Amazon Fresh)

Cainiao, Alibaba's logistics arm, can be likened to Amazon Logistics, ensuring efficient delivery. 

Its Digital Media and Entertainment group encompasses Alibaba Pictures and Youku, one of China's largest video streaming sites, (Equivalent to YouTube.)

It's worth noting that Alibaba has around 33% ownership of Ant Group. This investment could be valued around $70-100 billion in the US.

Alibaba's consistent revenue generation and its stock repurchase program. ($45 billion dollars trough 2025), which reduces outstanding shares, indicate a positive outlook. The potential catalyst of the Ant IPO could lead to significant growth in stock price.

The current price of Alibaba may be influenced by fear, uncertainty, and doubt, but I believe it to be more of a propaganda-driven sentiment.

Considering the relaxed China/US relationship and the possibility of a bullish market, Alibaba has the potential to move higher in the coming months or years.

While risks exist, the potential rewards outweigh them.

(This was /is never a short term play)

2 cents.￼￼

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/b4v/dollarbaba*

### Post #64: 2023-08-29 19:52 — `$NFLX`

**Compound war**

Hi All,

I’ll play this game/challenge called the “compounding war”. 

Started this account with about $13,700 ( in honor of Takashi Kotegawa) in $NFLX on 8/23/2023. The game consists of entering a specific security for an hour, a day or even a month and compound percentages ( it could be only 1%- or +15% increase per trade…or decrease) with the only goal to compound the gainz and see where it takes me! I do believe that if you are great at trading with small amount$, you will be great at trading with larger  amount$.. 

I’ll also be doing all in a stock, however, I will not be playing earnings unless very high conviction nor doing high risk stuff... I will playing uptrend/downtrend and go with the wave as well as sitting in cash when I can’t find a good setup.

Decided to start this now since I think this platform has everything to make it more fun!

🎢  

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/cqh/compound-war*

### Post #69: 2023-09-14 02:28 — `$NVAX`

**Asked chatgpt about $NVAX**

What are  possible outcomes for a stock that is owned %60.91 by institutions and have %46.11 short interest with only 83.93 million float


The possible outcomes for a stock with 60.91% institutional ownership, 46.11% short interest, and a float of only 83.93 million shares can vary depending on market sentiment, news, and trading activity. Here are some potential scenarios:

1. Short Squeeze: If positive news or developments related to the stock emerge, it could trigger a short squeeze. Short sellers may need to buy back shares to cover their positions, driving the stock price higher.

2. Volatility: With a high level of short interest and relatively low float, the stock may experience increased volatility. Rapid price swings can occur, making it attractive to traders but also risky.

3. Institutional Influence: Institutional investors often have a significant impact on stock prices. If they decide to buy or sell more shares, it can influence the stock's direction.

4. Market Sentiment: Market sentiment plays a crucial role. Positive sentiment can drive the stock price up, while negative sentiment can lead to declines.

5. Liquidity Concerns: The low float can result in limited liquidity, meaning that buying or selling large quantities of shares may be challenging without significantly affecting the stock price.


6. Short-term Speculation: Traders may speculate on short-term price movements due to the high short interest. However, this can be risky, as short-term moves can be unpredictable.

### Post #112: 2023-12-22 12:33

**My hunt for the next big move**

Blink Charging shows impressive financial growth with a revenue surge of over 100%, maintaining a steady 27% margin. Despite its substantial potential, the market cap remains surprisingly low. Their app ranks 32nd on the Apple Store for navigation, and recent spikes in volume follow strong earnings last quarter .

While heavily shorted (over 30%), the balance sheet looks healthy with minimal debt. Cash on hand equals a third of the debt. February marks the next earnings report.

Recent highlights include a 7-year agreement to be the official charging provider of Miami Beach, winning a contract in Utah, and plans for expansion into Latin America. Despite short-seller concerns, the business model seems sustainable in the growing EV market.

Bankruptcy seems unlikely, especially with positive developments on the horizon. 

Will open a position today to ride it up for a bit. 
But will use my other brokerage for this ( unfortunately not supported yet)

Going on vacation tomorrow, so will disconnect for about 2 weeks(will not be able to paper-hand like I did with RKLB)

**Anyone likes to play poker ?♥️ ♦️ ♣️ 🃏 **

The stock market resembles a game of poker. You're dealt a hand and can assess the situation once the flop is revealed before significant bets are made. Your decision-making revolves around evaluating your hand (the stock you're observing) and the flop (the known market and data) to determine if it's worthwhile. Then you have the turn and then the river ( your catalysts) It's essential to aim for a higher probability of success and avoid going all-in unless you're confident you have the best hand available.

### Post #156: 2024-03-21 18:42 — `$AAPL`

**DOJ claim review $AAPL **

Including my comments under each DOJ claim…



Excluding Cross-Platform Messaging Apps. Apple has made the quality of cross-platform messaging worse, less innovative, and less secure for users so that its customers have to keep buying iPhones.

My comments: There is no definitive evidence to support the statement that Apple intentionally makes cross-platform messaging worse, less innovative, or less secure to compel users to purchase iPhones. Apple's messaging services, such as iMessage, are proprietary and primarily designed for Apple devices to provide a seamless user experience within their ecosystem. Also, I don't even use iMessage or don't even care about it as an end user. lol.. What does role does whatsapp plays here? ….



Blocking Innovative Super Apps. Apple has disrupted the growth of apps with broad functionality that would make it easier for consumers to switch between competing smartphone platforms.

My comments: While some argue that Apple's strict guidelines hinder the development and distribution of innovative "super apps" that could potentially facilitate switching between smartphone platforms, others contend that Apple's regulations are necessary for maintaining security, privacy, and user experience standards within its ecosystem.  IMO, I prefer apple over others mainly because user security.


Suppressing Mobile Cloud Streaming Services. Apple has blocked the development of cloud-streaming apps and services that would allow consumers to enjoy high-quality video games and other cloud-based applications without having to pay for expensive smartphone hardware.

My comments: Apple's guidelines have been perceived as restrictive, particularly in regard to cloud gaming services, as they require each game available through such services to be individually listed on the App Store and subject to review, which some developers argue is impractical and limits the potential of cloud gaming platforms. This is always evolving, but does this look like a monopolistic practice?


Diminishing the Functionality of Non-Apple Smartwatches. Apple has limited the functionality of third-party smartwatches so that users who purchase the Apple Watch face substantial out-of-pocket costs if they do not keep buying iPhones.

My comments:  Really? do I need to comment? it is starting to look ridiculous.


Limiting Third Party Digital Wallets. Apple has prevented third-party apps from offering tap-to-pay functionality, inhibiting the creation of cross-platform third-party digital wallets.



My comments: This has evolved, if you don't believe me look  Venmo contactless payment . Square also has the same capabilities. 




In summary, I believe this DOJ will benefit Apple as a company in the long run and the decrease in price is a nothingburger.. if you do not believe me. look at this post in a month or two.  ( Current $AAPL = $171.00)

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/dRy/doj-claim-review-dollaraapl*

-He sees a major event coming in the company  very soon (maybe June 13th? ). I believe that this has to do with GME raising capital… what are they going to do with it? Is  it that important?


-He sees the company raising 2B cash with almost no debt a good/healthy thing to do. He was not sure if the company is raising more cash with shares dilution in the near future, but it looks like he doesn’t think that this going to negatively impact the company/business.. in fact, he started building  call  position when gme issued the first 45 million share offering on May 17th… (so I personally believe this is one factor that made RK so bullish with so short dated options)👀


I’ve watched all RK videos since his first appearance in 2021. And for those who have followed him, you can see he seemed very happy/confident with his position  and it felt the same as any of the videos he recorded in the past… people that haven’t watched him will feel he is an idiot/weirdo and doesn’t know what he is doing.

### Post #224: 2024-09-14 18:22

**Poker ♥️ ♦️ ♣️ and Stock Market 📈 📉 **

Just got inspired about 6 similarities of both; Market and Poker… and wanted to shared the following writing .

Both poker and stock market investing require a combination of skill, strategy, risk management, and an ability to make decisions based on incomplete information. In both, there is an element of luck in the short term, but long-term success depends on the ability to think strategically, manage resources, and adapt to new information.


 1. **Risk and Reward**:

   - **Poker**: Players constantly weigh the potential rewards of a hand against the risks of losing. They must decide whether to fold, call, or raise based on incomplete information about other players’ cards. You rarely go all in.

   - **Stock Market**: Investors balance potential returns with risk. They decide when to buy, sell, or hold based on incomplete information about the market, companies, and economic conditions.


 2. ** Asymmetry**:

   - **Poker**: Players never have full knowledge of their opponents' cards. Success depends on interpreting partial information, Probability,reading opponents' behaviors, and making educated guesses.

   - **Stock Market**: Investors never have perfect information. They rely on financial reports, market trends, and external factors but must accept that some information is hidden or unknown, such as insider knowledge.


3. **Bluff and Psychology **:

   - **Poker**: Bluffing is a central part of the game, where players create a false perception of their hand's strength to influence others' decisions.

   - **Stock Market**: Market sentiment and psychology play huge roles. Investors may follow trends, be influenced by market hype, or overreact to news, sometimes driving stock prices away from their true value. Learn to detect when the market is bluffing you :-)


4. **Money Management**:

   - **Poker**: Bankroll management is crucial. Skilled players manage how much they bet in each hand, ensuring they can withstand losses and maximize gains over the long run.

   - **Stock Market**: Successful investors manage their capital wisely, ensuring they don’t overexpose themselves.



5. **Patience and Discipline**:

   - **Poker**: Good players know when to fold and wait for better opportunities, exercising patience and discipline.

   - **Stock Market**: Smart investors don’t chase every opportunity and know when to hold off, sticking to their investment strategy rather than reacting emotionally to short-term fluctuations.


6. **Adapt**:

   - **Poker**: As the game progresses, players must constantly adapt their strategies based on new information and changing dynamics at the table.

   - **Stock Market**: Investors need to adjust to new market trends, economic data, and company performance, staying flexible with their strategy to maximize opportunities.


I hope you find this information useful. 

💎💵💎













*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/hFN/poker-love-and-stock-market*

### Post #235: 2024-09-29 18:16 `[Poll]`

**What type of trader are you?**

### **Types of Traders**###

1. **Day Traders**  
   - Buy and sell financial instruments within the same day, closing all positions before the market closes to avoid overnight risks.

2. **Swing Traders**  
   - Hold trades for several days or weeks, attempting to profit from short- to medium-term price moves.

3. **Position Traders**  
   - Take long-term positions in the market, often holding investments for months or even years, focusing on fundamental trends rather than daily price fluctuations.

4. **Momentum Traders**  
   - Seek to capitalize on trends by buying assets that are showing upward momentum and selling them when the momentum weakens, or vice versa for downward trends.

Note that you can be all at different points in life..  But there’s one that defines you. Which one is it?

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/h05/what-type-of-trader-are-you*

In 2024, the four fiscal quarters of the financial markets follow the typical calendar structure. Here's when each quarter start/end date:

1. **Q1 (First Quarter):**
   - **Start:** January 1, 2024
   - **End:** March 31, 2024

2. **Q2 (Second Quarter):**
   - **Start:** April 1, 2024
   - **End:** June 30, 2024

3. **Q3 (Third Quarter):**
   - **Start:** July 1, 2024
   - **End:** September 30, 2024

4. **Q4 (Fourth Quarter):**
   - **Start:** October 1, 2024
   - **End:** December 31, 2024

### Post #249: 2024-10-04 03:09 — `$MU` `[Dd]`

**Optionistics! And the small $OXY trade**

Ok, so I wanted to share a small resource that may help some options traders or amateurs (like me) to make more informed decisions before a trade:

https://www.optionistics.com/quotes/option-prices

This is very powerful as it allows you to quickly see important information on any option chain. ( OI, volatility and Volume over a period of time.)

For example, in the $OXY call $55 11/55 call option you can see an increase in Volume and OI doubled around Sept 23th . What may have caused that? See: (September 23 to 27, 2024 – Israel kills more than 700 people in Lebanon) https://www.aljazeera.com/amp/news/2024/10/2/timeline-the-key-moments-that-led-to-irans-missile-attacks-on-israel

Yesterday,  the Goldman Sachs “Analyst” downgraded the stock, but it did not work…  (similar to what happend to $MU the other day…) I must say that thanks to this downgrade , I was able to get few calls, very cheap. .. I knew this downgrade had high probability to be BS, specially since WarrenBuffet bought Billions at +$57.. So to me, a strike price of $55 looks very sexy too😂🤣

https://finance.yahoo.com/news/occidental-petroleum-given-neutral-rating-133140046.html

http://www.openinsider.com/search?q=Oxy

Please note that one data by itself, may not mean a lot, but when you combine different info: (chart, trend, valuation, option data, institutional buying  and world events) you can make better and more informed decisions for options. 

Whats next: Well, we have a lot of uncertainty in middle east, and as much as I don’t want war, there is nothing i can do to stop it. 

Earnings is on 11/12 and even though im not holding even near to expiration, option contracts tend to get more expensive as volatility arises when earnings approach… Trending up + uncertain = high volatility and expensive call contracts ..

For example, the call contract i bought, is priced for $OXY to not be above $56.66 by 11/12… even if the share price stays flat, the contract may cost a lot more near 11/12 since it will price a higher % move (up/down) 

In the past $OXY  avg priced in around %9 move during earnings date…this alone is $5 per contact if it stays $55 near 11/12.


I hope this inspires and help other traders out there 🫡 
 
Good night 🌙 


*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/ieo/optionistics-and-the-small-dol*

### Post #259: 2024-10-06 12:30 — `$AIQ, $GNOM, $RXRX, $EDOC`

**Recent Event 💡 👀**

This is a personal story that happened to me very recent ( in fact a few hours ago)—not related to the stock market—but it made me reflect on where we, as humans, are heading and how things have evolved.

I went to the ER because of hives/allergies (my first experience with this), and I had no idea what triggered it. The day I noticed it, I wasn’t even sure what it was, so I turned to ChatGPT for help by sending some photos and asking what it might be.

Of course, ChatGPT isn’t a doctor, but it provided a description and four possible causes. I ruled out the ones I knew weren’t relevant and was left with allergies.

Next, I asked for examples of medications that might help based on the photo. It suggested two: loratadine (Claritin) and diphenhydramine (Benadryl).

To make a long story short, I eventually went to the ER because the hives were worsening. Once there, a young nurse gave me steroids and Benadryl. When I asked her what the difference was between Benadryl and Claritin, she hesitated and finally admitted, “Actually, I’m not really sure what the difference is.”

My brain exploded. I thought, “How could a nurse not know this?” It made me wonder if we’re in trouble or if the world seriously needs to change… probably both.

Later, I opened the AH app, and the first post I saw at 5:00 AM EST was from Neverpullover about buying $RXRX. I didn’t know much about them, but I’d seen many ( probably a thousand 🤣😂) posts from SqueezeKid about it, so I decided to read a little more.

In brief, It turns out that Recursion is a biotech company using AI and machine learning to accelerate the discovery of treatments for various diseases. That really struck a chord with me and got me thinking about how AI could  or is transforming the healthcare industry.

I really don’t know any potential near term catalyst for this or if there are other AI bio stocks or ETFs out there.. but definitely something society will benefit from .

PS: of course I asked ChatGPT mentioned a few ETFs with AI and bio in it:  

$EDOC
$AIQ
$GNOM

Im have not  invested or traded this sector, but merits more exploration.

Should I buy some ai bio? @squeezeekid @neverpullover
🤭

### Post #287: 2024-10-11 20:16 — `$OXY, $SQ, $RXRX, $BA, $HOOD, $TSLA, $MSTR` `[News]`

**🚀 The Stock Market’s Wild Week 🎢 October 11 2024**

Buckle up, because this week in the stock market felt like riding a Tesla robotaxi!. If you like this post it let me know as I don’t mind creating 52 of these posts a year :-) . Maybe select 5 AH trending stock tickers during  each  week? @Sirjack is there a way for us to find this info :-) 

Let’s start with what I own…

$SQ (Square/Block): 💳 💻Square had a positive week after completing a major infrastructure shift, basically giving itself a superhero cape in the world of payments. Now, you can start a tab, order ahead, or maybe even buy a drink for your favorite influencer—all through Square's shiny new Orders Platform. The tech’s getting faster, sellers are happy, and customers are drinking more.

And of course, it wouldn’t be a week in the stock market without some Bitcoin drama. ($MSTR), which seems to be in a committed relationship with Bitcoin. Since 2020, they’ve been hoarding Bitcoin like it’s Black Friday and they’re the only ones with a cart. With a cool 252,220 BTC in their digital piggy bank, valued at over $15 billion, MicroStrategy is officially the largest corporate Bitcoin holder. Their CEO is all-in on Bitcoin’s long-term potential and even thinks it could hit $13 million per coin by 2045—because why dream small? @token …We also have a new AH millionaire today thanks to bitcoin🥳

Now, you might be thinking, "Okay, but what does this have to do with Square?" Well, don’t forget that Square’s very own Jack Dorsey is a massive Bitcoin fanboy. While $SQ isn’t fully riding the Bitcoin wave like MicroStrategy, the connection to Bitcoin helps influence its stock, especially as people continue to speculate.

Block has been giving Wall Street pleasant surprises like your favorite restaurant giving you extra fries. Earnings have consistently beaten estimates, and with positive predictions for the next quarter, this stock might just be the dessert you're looking for in the fintech space.

$OXY (Occidental Petroleum): 🛢️ Occidental Petroleum's week could be described as “not great, but still riding on Warren Buffett’s coattails.” With oil prices dropping faster than the temperature in your office AC, OXY is feeling the heat. But hey, when Buffett owns 28% of your stock, you can still walk with a bit of swagger. Plus, geopolitical tensions could boost oil prices again faster than you can say “crude.”

$RXRX (Recursion Pharmaceuticals): 🧬 🤖 Cathie Wood is doubling down on Recursion, and she's hoping her favorite AI-driven drug discovery company can whip up some magic potions. RXRX has been on a tech-biopharma merger spree, pairing up with Exscientia to create the Avengers of pharmaceutical research. It’s all AI, machine learning, and algorithms—sounds like the next sci-fi blockbuster, but with more billion-dollar drug opportunities on the horizon.

With partnerships and milestones lined up like a series of fortunate events, Recursion’s future looks bright, even if it’s not churning out profits just yet. @squeezekid believes in this company  and has great DD posts all over AH.

$BA (Boeing): ✈️ This week was a little turbulent for Boeing—newsflash: it's bad. When your biggest headline is something along the lines of “it’s not great,” you might want to avoid flying too close to the sun. Boeing's stock is like the middle seat on a budget airline—uncomfortable and filled with regret. But hey, there's always hope for a smoother landing. For investors that like planes, this is definitely on the watch list for weeks to come. If you do not believe be, ask @wTF

$HOOD (Robinhood): 🦊 Jim Cramer gave Robinhood a thumbs up this week, and the stock has been surging like GameStop in early 2021. Robinhood is attracting more assets than a kid collecting Pokémon cards, with custody assets now at $140 billion (double what they had last year). Plus, with crypto trading and global expansion, Robinhood’s been cashing in on every opportunity faster than you can say “diamond hands.”

Keep an eye on the company’s upcoming trader summit, where they’re expected to launch more trading features (Desktop?) I received a personal invitation from them :-). Anyways,  It looks like Robinhood is moving beyond its meme stock days and growing into something bigger and more serious. If you do not believe me, ask @WILLIS as he he all in on Robinhood lol.

$TSLA (Tesla): 🤖🚖 Tesla was all set to wow investors this week with the highly anticipated debut of its robotaxi, but... well, it fell a bit flat. The new "Cybercab" may look like something from a futuristic movie, but investors were less than thrilled, calling it a “toothless taxi.” Tesla stock took a hit, sinking 7% faster than Elon Musk’s latest tweet.

Still, it's Tesla, so you know there’s always a plot twist lurking around the corner. While the robotaxi isn't driving Tesla to new heights (yet), who knows—maybe in the future, we’ll all be hailing one of Musk’s creations on the way to the moon. 🌙

Make sure to keep your popcorn ready because next week could be even crazier. Stay invested, stay curious, and remember… in the stock market, sometimes it’s all about enjoying the ride! 🎢

### Post #289: 2024-10-12 02:33 — `$NFLX, $META, $MSFT, $AMZN, $AAPL, $GOOGL` `[Dd]`

**The evolution of money 🪙💵💳**

Money is set to undergo a profound transformation, driven by technologies like blockchain, artificial intelligence (AI), and the growing dominance of digital currencies.

Think about it…What will be the currency of use when Elon start sending people to other stars?🚀🪐 ✨ 

Do you recall the early days of the internet? Its introduction revolutionized every sector, and those who resisted its adoption were left behind. Even money wasn't immune to this change, as the internet facilitated cashless transactions across vast distances and remote locations(Square emerged as a result of this shift). 

A similar pattern is unfolding with AI. Broad adoption began only a few years ago, but it’s already clear: businesses and industries that fail to embrace AI will struggle to survive.

Money will follow this trend. The most natural way for AI and money to integrate seamlessly is through the blockchain.

How will money evolve? Here is what I have been thinking for some time….


1. Digital and Decentralized Currencies:

   - Cryptocurrencies: Bitcoin and government-issued central bank digital currencies (CBDCs) will become more mainstream and could potentially replace traditional forms of money in many regions. Blockchain-based currencies will allow for faster, more secure, and transparent transactions.

   - Programmable Money: AI could enable money that is programmable, meaning that it can execute certain rules and conditions automatically, like smart contracts. This would allow conditional payments to be processed seamlessly across different industries.


2. Cashless Societies:

   - AI is driving the trend toward  cashless economies, with mobile payments and digital wallets becoming increasingly prevalent. Physical cash may largely be phased out in many parts of the world, with transactions taking place through mobile, biometric, and AI-driven payment systems.


3. AI-Managed Financial Ecosystems:

   - AI is expected to automate many aspects of financial management. AI financial assistants could manage investments, savings, and expenditures autonomously, optimizing individuals' and businesses' finances in real-time based on personalized data analysis and market conditions.


4. Global Unified Financial Systems:

   - The idea of a  global digital currency that operates across borders is increasingly plausible. With AI and blockchain technologies, a unified global financial system could emerge, minimizing the need for foreign exchange markets or transaction fees, making global commerce more fluid and cost-effective.

5. AI-Powered Regulatory Systems:

   - Governments and financial institutions will likely rely on AI-driven regulatory systems to monitor and ensure compliance in financial transactions. These systems could automatically detect fraud, money laundering, and other illicit activities at speeds and accuracies far beyond human capability.

I firmly believe that companies in the financial sector that embrace AI and Blockchain will benefit the most during the next phase of modern money.

If you do not believe me, look at the companies that embraced the internet in the early days and truly developed their product around it. $AAPL, $GOOGL $AMZN $META $MSFT $NFLX(without the internet these companies wouldn’t be what they are… and do they really have competitors?)

Same will happen with AI and with Blockchain….

Inside Warrens 🧠 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/izH/the-evolution-of-money*

### Post #295: 2024-10-13 15:30 — `$SQ, $FOUR, $GME`

**Another tool ⚙️🔧🛠️  - Dataroma**

https://www.dataroma.com/m/holdings.php?m=SAM

Probably some of you already use something like this to peek into great investors' stock holdings (kinda like Afterhour, but with a few months delay 😅). 

What I find really useful is checking out sector and portfolio allocation, and seeing the prices prominent investors are paying for companies I'm interested in. It’s a great way to validate your own investment thesis!

For example, you can see that Burry dropped $SQ earlier this year and picked up $FOUR instead. When 13% of his portfolio is in one stock, it gives you a clue not just about that company, but also about the sector he’s betting on.

If you remember in the past, he also was ballz deep in $GME  (portfolio wise) a year before the squeeze and dropped it before the 🚀 took off.

For those who now Burry investment style, he does not hold a stock for long time ( no more than few quarters) and sees value were most can’t)

Hope some of you find the data in this tool useful !

### Post #326: 2024-10-18 20:13 — `$OXY, $NVDA, $SQ, $DRUG, $NFLX, $TSM, $OKLO, $AMD, $ADBE, $RXRX, $HOOD, $TSLA, $MSTR` `[News]`

**🚀Market Thrills: Crypto Mysteries, Biotech Breakthroughs, and Tech Stock Surges🎢 October 18 2024**

This week in the stock market felt like a thrilling roller coaster, with everyone strapping in tight and hoping not to lose their lunch. We saw everything from Bitcoin disappearing into the digital void, to Robinhood dressing up in fancy new clothes. Here’s a breakdown of some wild moments in the market, stock by stock—grab your popcorn!

Tesla ($TSLA)🤖🚖
Elon Musk might not be launching a new rocket, but he’s certainly launching a lot of Bitcoin into the unknown! On Tuesday, Tesla decided to move almost all of its $760 million in Bitcoin to mysterious wallets. Did Elon forget a password, or is he secretly building a crypto moon base? Either way, with Tesla’s BTC stash almost emptied out, crypto fanatics are left scratching their heads. The stock’s hovering around $220 and, as always, keeping everyone guessing what might happen next. Hint: it's probably best to buckle up before the next Tesla earnings. Kudos to @refinedwarrior for putting this on my radar.

Microstrategy ($MSTR) ₿🏦
$MSTR is still living its best life as Bitcoin prices surge to their highest since July. With crypto fanboys everywhere doing victory laps, the stock is enjoying the ride. Just remember, when Bitcoin sneezes, MicroStrategy catches a cold – or in this case, a fever. As long as Bitcoin keeps climbing, $MSTR is like the sidecar to the wild ride of crypto volatility. Just hold on tight! We have a lot of crypto fan boys out there @token @trollstein. Also, Bitcoin crossed 68K today.

Block ($SQ)💳 💻
Block is behaving like the ambitious climber that it is—no crazy headlines here, just steady ascent. Cathie Wood has been quietly selling shares, which is kind of like seeing the pilot leave the cockpit mid-flight. But don't panic, the stock still seems poised to hit $100 by year-end (according to optimistic believers). Let's hope it doesn't get stage fright after the election!

Recursion Pharmaceuticals ($RXRX)🧬 🤖
Science geeks are buzzing about Recursion Pharmaceuticals, with the company prepping for some big data drops on its potential treatments for neurofibromatosis and colon cancer. This stock could either cure your financial woes or, well…just give you more research to do. One thing’s for sure—biotech fanatics are keeping their microscopes trained on this one, waiting to see if it’s the next big breakthrough or just another test tube fizzle. Well, @squeezekid probably has more to say on this one than I do. @SirJack added $1,000 to the options play on this one (%0.01176 of his portfolio)

Occidental Petroleum ($OXY)🛢️
OXY is the stock equivalent of that one person at the party who refuses to leave, even after everyone else is gone. Oil prices are near their 52-week low, and so is OXY. But this company is no stranger to dramatic comebacks, especially when crude oil takes a turn upward. Add Warren Buffett's steady accumulation of shares into the mix, and you start wondering if Uncle Warren knows something we don’t. Right now, the stock’s feeling a bit under the weather, but we know it’ll perk up once the oil market does its cyclical thing.

Robinhood ($HOOD)🦊
Robinhood, the cool kid of meme stock mania, just revealed its new look. The company is throwing index options and futures into its trading app, like adding sprinkles to an already chaotic sundae. They even unveiled “Robinhood Legend,” which is basically the app's sleek, more mature older sibling—think Bollinger Bands and eight charts at once. While the stock itself might not be legendary yet, the new features sure could bring a spark (or at least a few thousand day traders ready to YOLO it all).Our friend @longwashere masterfully used options during the Hood event, exactly as they were intended, and managed to sweep cash from the stock market. He played both long, short, and everything in between. I pictured him like Zeus, taking down all the other Greeks and gods of volatility.

Semiconductor Stocks ($NVDA $AMD $TSM etc)🖥️🧠
Semiconductors were all over the place this week—one day they're down, the next day they're sprinting upward. TSMC’s strong sales forecast on Thursday had Nvidia, Broadcom, and others in the sector bouncing back after ASML’s gloomy outlook earlier in the week. Chip stocks are like that friend who constantly talks about their fitness gains—you’re impressed, but you can’t help but wonder when the next crash diet is coming.. Our friend @adioking journaled how he banked the semis move.

ADOBE ($ADBE)🎨
Added this to my watchlist as it approaches a strong support zone. It could either bounce on positive news or take a dive—choose your stance!

Bright Minds Biosciences ($DRUG) 💊
Congrats to @RLucky for verifiably having a winning position ( Shares) over %44 with this stock which is literally on drugs this week.

Oklo ($OKLO) ☢️⚡
Well, what can I say? Give this guy a round of applause and a follow @Moonopoly 

Netflix ($NFLX) 🎬🔴
Netflix just dropped a blockbuster earnings report, beating expectations and sending its stock soaring over 10% today. Analysts are loving it, with some boosting their price targets to as high as $850, calling it a "top pick" in streaming. Congrats to the #1 Netflix investor @dteich16

Overall, it’s been a wild ride across sectors, with everything from crypto, oil, biotech, and tech stocks making headlines. Remember, in the stock market, one minute you're riding high, and the next, you're clutching your seatbelt, trying to hold on. Enjoy the ride!

**China Technical Correction  🇨🇳**

It's no surprise to seasoned investors or traders to see that most major Chinese stocks corrected over 10% in just a few days. After such a rapid surge, it was only a matter of time. However, for me, this looks like a clear entry point…and for anyone planning to go long on China and hold for at least 12 months (Maybe DCA  every day for the next 2 months if you are conservative). 

If I weren't already in $SQ, I'd probably be looking at $BIDU, $BABA, or something broader like $FXI.

Why $BIDU ($94)? The answer is simple. If you're looking to invest in AI within an international market that hasn't yet priced in the potential of its stocks, $BIDU is the way to go. With a market cap just around $30 billion and similar cash reserves, it's doing buybacks and trading at the same valuation it had in 2010, right after the U.S. recession. $BABA could also be a strong AI contender, but if you're uncertain, $FXI might be a safer choice.

Take Palantir, for example. No one believed in it when it was trading at $7, even though its market cap seemed high relative to its revenue and earnings at the time. But AI winners are full of surprises!

### Post #334: 2024-10-20 02:12 — `$NVDA, $SQ, $AAPL`

**Anothet Tool🧰🔧🪛**


https://companiesmarketcap.com


Let’s be honest, without data, making informed decisions is nearly impossible. And if we can’t visualize the data clearly, it makes decision-making even more difficult. 

This tool is incredibly useful because it presents key information quickly and allows for faster visual analysis, especially if you're interested in diving into the financials of companies you're considering investing in. In comparison, websites like Yahoo Finance or broker platforms such as Thinkorswim often present the data in a more abstract way that doesn’t feel as intuitive to me. 

So, what can you see on this website? It provides historical graphs for key data points for companies, including:

- **Market capitalization**: How much a company is worth based on its stock price.

- **Revenue**: The money a company makes from selling its products or services.

- **Earnings**: The company’s profit after paying all its expenses.

- **Outstanding shares**: The total number of shares a company has given to people to own.

- **Available cash**: The money a company has that it can spend right now.

- **Debt**: The money a company owes and needs to pay back.

- **Total assets**: Everything a company owns that has value, like money or buildings.

- **Total liabilities**: Everything a company owes to others, like loans or bills.

- **Price-to-sales ratio (P/S)**: A way to compare a company’s stock price to how much money it makes from sales.

These are crucial metrics for any serious investor to evaluate.

In the screenshots below, check out the information for $SQ as an example.

Additionally, on the homepage, you can view the largest companies worldwide by market cap (Wow, $NVDA is closing in on $AAPL!). You can even sort the data by revenue or earnings.

There’s an abundance of data you can capture, filter, compare and sort to fit your needs! 

Hope you enjoy this!

Bookmark if you find this useful!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/iV4/anothet-tool*

**🚀The Stock Market’s Wild Week - October 25, 2024🎢**

This week was all about earnings and ambitious growth predictions! From Tesla’s soaring stock after an earnings beat to PureCycle’s impressive gains. Special thanks to our whale @shortsqueeze for posting this $PCT position everyday, every hour , every minute since he started it..Only very wise people followed him on this play!.. Please go ahead and follow this user as he proves that he is very serious by putting $2Million chips (YOLO) and up over %50 as of this writing!…..Ok, so moving forward,  let’s dive in. 📊

$TSLA  🤖🚖:
Tesla just zoomed  over 18% higher after crushing its Q3 earnings! With Elon Musk hinting at 20-30% growth for 2025, it seems like the Cybercab future is still on track despite the robotaxi not quite thrilling everyone. Musk is betting on autonomy and AI to make Tesla the most valuable company in the world. Will it reach those lofty goals? Investors seem to think so (for now). I was able to open a $220 Jan 2025 call 9 days ago and got lucky ... .I saw how Tesla dropped like %20 from its high after the robot event, and was like…ok this is a good one entering the earnings and the election. 🚀 Sold for %150 gain…Then gambled $3K this morning of the earnings in puts, 1 month away ( just betting on a small but sharp decline).. $SQ 💳 💻:

I have a strong conviction on this one for 2025 and any shares below $66 before earnings, I’ll scoop it up.

$MSTR  ₿🏦:
Peter Schiff is predicting doom for MicroStrategy and its Bitcoin stash, but with Bitcoin still hanging around and MSTR stock up +20% this week, it's looking pretty good—for now. Who knows what the future holds for Bitcoin? Maybe all we need is a catalyst—Trump’s back in the news in two weeks...hmm 🤔. Wait, we also have $MSFT involved in the bitcoin news today!$MSFT Microsoft shareholders are gearing up for a spicy vote on December 10 that’ll pique Bitcoin lovers' interest. Nope, this isn’t a board-led initiative; a conservative think tank is behind the push, wanting Microsoft to at least consider Bitcoin as an investment hedge.

So, will this vote open a Bitcoin window at Microsoft, or is it just a teaser? Stay tuned, crypto fans!

https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/789019/000119312524242884/d878959ddefa14a.htm

$HOOD  🦊:

$RXRX )🧬 🤖:
Not the best week for Recursion Pharmaceuticals, but biotech is like that. Stay tuned, though, because the AI-powered drug discovery space is still exciting, and Recursion got big partnerships in play. Biotech moves slow but packs a punch when it does 📈.

$NVDA 🖥️🧠:
Nvidia hit another 52-week high! AI and semiconductors are still ruling the tech world, and with Q3 earnings around the corner, investors are expecting more good news from the chip giant. Don’t sleep on Nvidia—it’s going from strength to strength 💪.

$ADBE 🎨:
Adobe’s facing some tough questions: will AI be its friend or foe? On the one hand, AI-powered design tools are threatening to undercut its core business, but on the other, Adobe is launching AI features to stay in the game. With analysts split, this could either be a masterpiece or a messy sketch. I still believe it is in a very strong support zone $485 for a swing ( 2 months period)Time will tell if Adobe’s future is bright or blurry. 🎨🤖

$PCT ♻️:
PureCycle was the real gem this week in AH, soaring over 35%! The company secured a $90 million investment, giving it the cash flow needed to ramp up its revolutionary recycling tech. With its Ironton facility gaining traction and production goals being met, PureCycle is transitioning from a pre-revenue company to a commercial operation. Big things are on the horizon for this recycling powerhouse. Hats down to @shortsqueeze  ♻️🚀.

$CPRI 👜: Ouch! Capri’s shares (Versace Parent Company)  nosedived 50% after a judge blocked its $8.5 billion merger with Tapestry. Turns out, pairing Versace, Michael Kors, and Coach in one luxury mega-club didn’t sit well with the FTC, who feared it would tighten the belt on competition and hike prices.So what’s next? Capri and Tapestry are appealing, claiming there’s plenty of room on the runway—even with Europe’s LVMH dominating the catwalk. Stay tuned for the next episode in this designer drama! Is this a buy the dip opportunity? Puts sellers are going to love it! Anyone going long on this one?


Additional notes:

Our biggest whale @wTF and his best friend, Martin did not think twice to play an option strategy position after $TSLA skyrocketed over %20! It looks like he is most likely bearshish in the near term , but selling options to fund some $ for this play. ( Taking advantage of IV decreases over time) …very fancy stuff… I'm grabbing popcorn to see how this evolves.

### Post #396: 2024-10-31 00:01

**“Catch the stop loss” by Mr Market 📊**

Once upon a time in the bustling world of the financial markets, there was a mischievous character named Mr. Market. Mr. Market was known for his unpredictable nature—he loved to move prices up and down just when traders least expected it. One of his favorite pastimes was playing a game called “Catch the Stop Loss.”

One morning, Mr. Market noticed a group of traders carefully setting their stop losses, each aiming to protect their gains or minimize their losses. To Mr. Market, stop losses were like invisible treasure chests scattered across the price chart. He rubbed his hands together, grinning with excitement, ready to make his move.

He started with small, subtle price dips, lulling the traders into a false sense of security. Prices began to drop, inching closer and closer to the traders’ stop loss points. Just when the traders thought the price might bounce back, Mr. Market threw in a sudden spike downward, catching one stop loss after another like a fisherman with a big net.

“Oh, no!” the traders gasped as they watched their positions get closed automatically, just as Mr. Market grinned wider. But, as quickly as he had dipped, he turned the price right back up again, leaving those who had been stopped out frustrated, watching the market move back up without them.

Mr. Market chuckled to himself, enjoying the chaos he had created. “Better luck next time!” he whispered to the traders, who were already busy adjusting their stop losses, hoping next time they’d outsmart Mr. Market.

And so, the game continued, with Mr. Market always on the lookout, ever ready to catch another stop loss by surprise.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/jCO/catch-the-stop-loss-by-mr-mark*

His latest master move? A $TSLA put options  + a play with Tesla betting it will be stuck between $210 and $300—earning him cash every time it wobbles in that zone. ### Post #412: 2024-11-02 01:28

**Who doesn’t like tools ⚒️ 🧰?**

Alright, newbies, gather ‘round! Today, I’m spilling some insider tea so you won’t go running to sell every time the news screams “market crash!” Here’s a nifty (and free!) tool: http://www.openinsider.com/charts. Sure, there are fancier ones, but why not keep it simple?

Check out the insider buy vs. sell charts—notice those spikes in 2008 and 2020? Yep, those were market bottoms and VIX peaks, the very moments when insiders were doubling or tripling up on buys compared to sells. Most people missed this cue, panicked, and missed the opportunity.

So next time you see that big blue spike (buys at least 2X the sells) and the news is yelling “economic doom,” consider going all in on SPY or a similar ETF if you’re playing it safe. Trust me, you’ll be setting yourself up to cash in!

Enjoying the weekend!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/jMz/who-doesnt-like-tools*

### Post #453: 2024-11-09 16:44

**⚙️🛠️ Tool for AH   / Margin = Debt⚙️🛠️**

Think of this less like a tool and more like an ultimate stock market cheat sheet. If you’re putting money into the market, you don’t just want to know about margin—you want to know how to make it your best friend (and sometimes your frenemy).

Now, maybe I’ve hinted at this before, but buckle up! We’re diving into some juicy data and eye-opening insights.

Margin 101: If “margin” sounds like a scary word, well, maybe stick to a savings account. But if you don’t know how margin moves the market valuations up or down…run, don’t just walk away from investing.

Here’s the scoop: FINRA dishes out monthly stats on margin use (the total of all debit balances in securities margin accounts), so you can download this data, pop it into a spreadsheet, and go wild with your analysis.

Website:
https://www.finra.org/rules-guidance/key-topics/margin-accounts/margin-statistics

CSV  of all data until now:

https://www.finra.org/sites/default/files/2021-03/margin-statistics.xlsx

What’s in it for you?

Picture this: with margin insights (plus the other goody I’ve shared: (https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/jMz/who-doesnt-like-tools) You’ll be primed to spot when we’re riding high in a bull market and potentially spot the top📈 or tumbling into bear territory 📉 or spot the bottom.

How can margin data be a crystal ball? Take a look back—2000, 2008, 2021. Notice how margin usage spiked and then hit the floor during those infamous times? The market’s peak and bottom were a game of margin. Remember the movie “Margin Call”?

So, what now? While pinpointing the exact top or bottom is tricky—thanks, money printing!—one thing’s for sure: each crash’s margin bottom ends up higher than the last… and each margin’s top ends higher than the last one (around 2x?). If things stay on trend, we’ve got another~ $600 million in margin before the next big market shake-up and downturn begins . ( of course with a margin of error!) Maybe if we know M2  and blend the data together we would have a better estimate? 🤔

Between potential rate cuts and the Trump comeback, the formula is ripe for the market to continue next year. But hey, nothing’s ever roses forever—brace yourself for the bumpy ride up before the bumpy ride down.

I hope this has opened your eyes and you have learned something valuable and new.

PS: I didn’t learn this in school.

Like, share and most importantly bookmark!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/khH/tool-for-ah-margin-debt*

### Post #471: 2024-11-15 01:51 — `$NKE`

**Trading Idea 💡 $NKE**

Bill Ackman went with $NKE this past Q: https://www.dataroma.com/m/holdings.php?m=psc

Here are some strategies to consider:

Just a heads up—this is Bill’s second purchase of NKE. The first time, he bought around 3 million shares, and now he’s increased his position by buying 13 million shares at an average price of $88. After his initial purchase, the stock rose from $72 to $88. Currently, the stock is around $76, pulling back with the overall market.

What actions could you take?

	1.	Sell Puts: You could sell puts with an expiration before the next earnings date to capture premium. For example, the Dec 6th $76 put pays $2. If you’re comfortable owning the stock at $76, this could be a way to earn some “free” income.

	2.	Credit Spreads: If you prefer credit spreads, consider the $75/$76 spread expiring Dec 13th. This pays over 80% if the stock remains above $76, which is a solid return. (Tip: you don’t have to hold until expiration. You can exit earlier if the stock stays flat or rises, even if you don’t want the full 80% return.) Tomorrow, this spread may pay less than 80%, but even a 50% return could be attractive.

Looking ahead, the next FOMC meeting is Dec 18th, and the Fed is expected to cut rates by 25 bps. Even if the market experiences some fear and pullbacks due to recent highs, I still believe we’re in a bull market. These pullbacks could ultimately be buying opportunities.

Hope this idea have opened a 💡 in you🤭

### Post #518: 2024-11-28 14:32 — `$BIDU, $BABA` `[Dd]`

**Baidu: The master splinter of  🇨🇳 tech stocks and AI**

Let’s talk about Baidu, the heavyweight champion of Chinese tech, often called the “Google of China.” But Baidu isn’t just a search engine; it’s a kung fu master punching its way into the future with AI, autonomous driving, and a booming cloud business. If you’re looking for a stock that could potentially roundhouse kick your portfolio to new heights by 2026, BIDU deserves a serious look.

Search Engine Supreme:👑

Baidu dominates China’s search market. With over 60% market share, it’s the go-to platform for over a billion internet users. 

But here’s the kicker: unlike Google, Baidu’s search business still has room to grow in a market that’s increasingly digitizing. That’s like finding a treasure map with half the gold still buried.

Autonomous Driving -The “Apollo” Mission:🧑‍🚀

While others are still arguing over EV charging stations, Baidu’s Apollo project is busy teaching cars to drive themselves. This isn’t just a moonshot, it’s a rocket 🚀 aimed at disrupting the $200 billion autonomous vehicle market in China.

Apollo is already partnered with over 200 companies and operates RoboTaxi fleets in major Chinese cities. By 2026, Baidu could have a fleet of robot chauffeurs making Elon Musk jealous. (Guess who will Tesla rely on for Autonomous driving in china? - data privacy etc.)

AI and Cloud -Baidu’s Hidden Dragon:🤖☁️

Baidu isn’t just a search engine; it’s becoming an AI powerhouse. Its AI Cloud business grew 6% YoY in Q3 2024, and it’s a key player in China’s AI revolution. 

With China heavily investing in AI and digital transformation, Baidu’s cloud and AI services could explode.

Financials -The Solid Foundation:🏦

In Q3 2024, Baidu reported:
•Revenue of 37.1 billion yuan, up 4% YoY.
•Net income of 7.63 billion yuan, up 14%.

While others are flailing in a tough market, Baidu’s efficiency and profitability are a testament to its mastery of the business game.

Valuation -The Underdog of the Tech World:🐕

With a P/S ratio of 1.59, BIDU is trading like a discount Rolex. Compare that to Western tech stocks with P/S ratios of 5, 10, or even 20, and you realize Baidu might be one of the most undervalued gems in the tech world.

2026 Price Target: $200

Here’s why this is realistic:

-AI Boom: As China’s AI and digital economy continue to grow, Baidu’s cloud and AI segments could drive double-digit growth.

-Autonomous Vehicles: With Apollo gaining traction, this could add a whole new revenue stream.

-Market Revaluation: As the market regains confidence in Chinese tech, BIDU’s P/S could easily expand to 2.5-3x, reflecting its growth potential.


Baidu isn’t just a stock, it’s a master of the digital kung fu world. Between its dominance in search, pioneering autonomous driving, and rapidly growing AI business, BIDU is ready to spar with the best in the global tech dojo. So, if you’re looking to add a little kung fu kick to your portfolio, Baidu might just be your ticket to a 2026 victory lap.


Why Trump Could Be Good for China Stocks?🇨🇳

At first glance, Trump and China might sound like oil and water. However, there are reasons why his return could be a boost for Chinese stocks, such as Alibaba ($BABA) and Baidu ($BIDU):

-Business-Friendly Policies:👯‍♂️

Trump is a businessman at heart, and his administration focused on creating favorable conditions for trade and commerce. While tensions with China were high, his policies often emphasized deals, not decoupling. A return to transactional diplomacy might lead to renewed trade negotiations benefiting Chinese companies operating globally.

-Lower Regulatory Pressure:📜

Under the current administration, U.S.-China relations have remained tense, and Chinese stocks have faced increased scrutiny and regulatory hurdles (e.g., delisting threats). Trump’s focus on economic outcomes rather than aggressive regulation could ease the pressure on Chinese companies listed in the U.S.

-Tariff Recalibration:📄

Trump loves tariffs, but he also loves striking “better deals.” A recalibration of tariffs or a more pragmatic approach to trade could benefit industries dependent on U.S.-China cooperation, bolstering investor confidence in Chinese equities.

-Market Sentiment: ❤️

Trump’s return could lead to a more predictable U.S.-China relationship built on negotiation and compromise. Markets hate uncertainty, and a clearer path forward might make investors more willing to bet on Chinese growth stories.

While Trump’s rhetoric may be tough, his focus on deal-making and economic growth could indirectly support Chinese stocks by reducing regulatory and geopolitical uncertainty, giving these companies room to thrive in global markets

### Post #519: 2024-11-28 17:38 — `$SQ, $MARA, $COIN, $HOOD, $MSTR`

**21K on Thanksgiving  🦃**

Getting to 21K made me think about bitcoin maximum supply which  will be 21M …so I had the following post queued up as I started brainstorming what would likely happen after bitcoin reaches this milestone:

As Bitcoin’s supply reaches its hard cap of 21 million coins, the dynamics of the ecosystem will shift significantly. This scarcity could drive new opportunities for companies involved in the broader cryptocurrency and blockchain space. 


1. Cryptocurrency Exchanges

Coinbase ($COIN) , ($HOOD) and other major exchanges will benefit from increased trading volume and higher transaction fees as Bitcoin scarcity drives price volatility and trading activity.


2. Bitcoin Mining and Staking Services

As mining rewards shrink to zero, mining companies like Marathon Digital Holdings ($MARA) will pivot to transaction fees as a revenue source. Their focus will likely shift toward making operations more energy-efficient to maintain profitability.

3. Blockchain Infrastructure Providers

Firms like Block ($SQ)  and MicroStrategy ($MSTR) with significant Bitcoin holdings or integrated Bitcoin payment systems will gain from the rising value of Bitcoin.

Blockchain technology providers like NVIDIA (producing GPUs for mining) and AMD could benefit indirectly through their involvement in decentralized application and infrastructure development.

4. Payment Processors and Financial Services

Companies like PayPal, Visa, and Mastercard that enable Bitcoin transactions could see growth as Bitcoin adoption becomes mainstream due to its limited supply and increasing value.

Emerging decentralized finance (DeFi) platforms could gain traction, offering services such as Bitcoin lending and borrowing.

5. Custodial and Asset Management Firms

Firms like Fidelity Digital Assets, Grayscale Investments, and Galaxy Digital may attract more institutional and retail investors looking to hold Bitcoin as a digital store of value.

Secure custodial solutions will become more crucial as Bitcoin becomes scarcer and its value climbs.

6. Security and Insurance Providers

Companies offering blockchain security and cryptocurrency insurance providers will benefit as the need for secure storage and transaction monitoring rises alongside Bitcoin’s value.

7. Alternative Energy Companies

With mining profitability reliant on transaction fees, mining operations will depend on cost-effective, sustainable energy sources. 

The hard cap on Bitcoin supply is expected to amplify its scarcity and demand dynamics, creating ripple effects across various industries. Companies directly involved in cryptocurrency trading, mining, blockchain infrastructure, and digital payments stand to gain the most. Additionally, businesses innovating within decentralized finance and sustainable energy will play an increasingly vital role in the ecosystem.

### Post #523: 2024-12-01 19:15

**Who am I and why am I here?**


Hi, I’m Warren Buffet. Well, not that Warren Buffett, let’s get that out of the way. I’m not the legendary “Oracle of Omaha,” but I did borrow his name because, honestly, who in the world of investing doesn’t know him? He’s the GOAT, the gold standard, the guy we all secretly want to be when we check our portfolios. 

When I first heard about Kevin building Afterhour, I was instantly hooked. The idea of a platform where you can see verified positions blew my mind. For years, the internet has been full of “trust me, bro” posts from anonymous profiles. But what if we could turn that noise into actionable insights? What if we could build a space where real investors, traders, and even the occasional degen could come together to share Due Diligence (DDs), discuss strategies, and grow wealth?

To me, Afterhour isn’t just a copy trades platform. It’s like some of the world’s greatest ideas, with a twist:

•Amazon isn’t a store—it’s a connector. It links you to millions of merchants who have what you need.

•Uber isn’t a taxi —it’s a system that ensures you’ll get where you’re going, when you need to go.

•Airbnb is not a house—but it puts you in touch with people who do have them, in almost any city around the world.

And now there’s Afterhour. It’s not a financial advisor; it doesn’t tell you what to do with your money. But it does connect you to the best investors, the sharpest minds, and the most promising trades out there….backed by verified positions. It’s a platform where ideas are more than words, and insights are backed by skin in the game.

For me, Afterhour is more than just a place to learn and copy trades. It’s a revolution in how we think about investing communities. It’s where degens meet disciplined investors, where the moonshots sit alongside the blue chips, and where everyone can find their edge.

And here’s the fun part: this isn’t just about stocks or crypto or options. It’s about people. The dreamers who think the next big thing is a click away. The grinders who read balance sheets like bedtime stories. The skeptics who’ve been burned before but are ready to try again. Afterhour is for all of us.

So, while I’m not the real Warren Buffet,I’d like to connect people , highlight opportunities, make smarter trades, and ultimately one way or another help you learn and grow. The world of investing is constantly changing , and so are you.

Because at the end of the day, investing isn’t just about numbers, it’s about building something bigger than yourself.

### Post #537: 2024-12-07 02:08

**What is FCF Yield? 💵**

The Free Cash Flow Yield is a financial metric that shows how much free cash flow a company generates relative to its market value. It is expressed as a percentage and helps investors understand how efficiently a company is generating cash in comparison to its current market price. In brief is like how much money of its own market cap 🧢 is making a year after paying all expenses, etc.

What Is Considered a High FCF Yield?

High FCF Yield: Above 8% is typically considered good.

Why it’s good: It means the company is generating a lot of cash compared to its stock price, which could make it a good value for investors. And yes, the real Warren likes  companies with good FCF yield + moat… who doesn’t?

eBay: Yep, I found a sweet little loophole with “eBay Bucks” and racked up some nice $$… until they shut it down. I may or may not have been the reason they changed the rules. 😂

PayPal: They banned me. Temporarily. Same reason. Nothing illegal…. just made too much money too fast. Turns out, “too good” is a thing. 😅

Amazon: Oh, they definitely have their systems in place. Sell too much too quickly, and BAM! Your account gets paused for review. Ask me how I know… because I’ve had a few of those pauses myself. 😂

Here’s the thing: big companies have these safeguards to make sure you’re not breaking the matrix (or their bottom line). I just found legal loopholes and pushed the boundaries a little… until they caught up and tweaked their systems. Honestly, I don’t blame them. It makes sense to protect their business , fair game.

### Post #543: 2024-12-08 23:50

**The 33/33/33 Rule for Success**


When it comes to trading, success is often misunderstood as purely being about the strategies or tools you use ( They are important). However, the reality is that trading is a balance of three critical components, each contributing equally to your results: Money Management, Psychology, and Market Cycles. Here’s how the 33/33/33 Rule can transform your approach to trading:

33% Money Management

Your ability to manage your capital is the foundation of long-term trading success. Even the best strategies will fail without proper risk management.

💎Position Sizing

💎Take-Profits

💎Risk-Reward Ratio: Aim for trades with at least a 2:1 reward-to-risk ratio to ensure profitability over time.

💡 Key Thought: Protect your capital first. The opportunity to trade tomorrow is more important than chasing today’s gains.

33% Psychology

Trading is as much about mastering your mindset as it is about understanding the markets. Emotions like fear and greed are the most common reasons traders fail.

💎Discipline: Stick to your trading plan no matter what.

💎Emotional Control: Avoid revenge trading after losses or overconfidence after wins.

💎Patience: The market doesn’t owe you anything..wait for high-probability setups.

💡 Key Thought:  You an your emotions are your greatest enemy in trading. Master your psychology, and you master the game.

33% Market Cycles

Markets move in cycles, and understanding these is crucial to timing your trades.

💎Trend Identification: Know whether the market is trending or consolidating.

💎Seasonality and Economic Cycles: Be aware of macroeconomic events, earnings seasons, and industry trends.

💎Technical Patterns: Use tools like Fibonacci retracements or Gann cycles to spot potential reversals and continuations.

💡 Key Thought: The market is cyclical, what goes up eventually comes down. Knowing where you are in the cycle can mean the difference between profit and loss.

Why the Balance Matters

Relying only on technical analysis or market cycles won’t save you if you lack risk management.

Even with excellent money management, poor psychology can lead to impulsive decisions.

Master your trading mindset, and you’ll have the patience to ride market cycles effectively.

Final Tip

Success in trading is not about being perfect; it’s about being balanced. The final %1  corresponds to evaluate your performance in each of these areas, and make sure you’re giving equal weight to Money Management, Psychology, and Cycles. Together, they make up the pillars of consistent profitability.

Are you ready to take control of all three aspects of trading? Let me know your thoughts in the comments! 🚀

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/o0a/the-333333-rule-for-success*

### Post #567: 2024-12-14 14:14 `[Discuss]`

**Satoshi Nakamoto’s famous quote**

 It was written on January 17, 2009, in an email to the Cryptography Mailing List announcing the release of Bitcoin version 0.1. In this email, Satoshi stated:

	“It might make sense just to get some in case it catches on. If enough people think the same way, that becomes a self-fulfilling prophecy.” 

https://satoshi.nakamotoinstitute.org/quotes/bitcoin-economics/

This remark reflects Satoshi’s perspective on the potential for Bitcoin’s adoption and the network effect, where the value of the network increases as more people participate.

This embedded message highlighted the financial instability at the time and underscored the motivation behind creating a decentralized currency like Bitcoin.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/qaP/satoshi-nakamotos-famous-quote*

### Post #569: 2024-12-15 00:00 `[Discuss]`

**Understanding $MSTR after inclusion**

MicroStrategy is now part of the Nasdaq index, which creates a cycle of demand. As investors buy MSTR shares to align with the index, Michael Saylor can issue more shares, using the proceeds to purchase additional Bitcoin. This drives up Bitcoin’s price, increasing MSTR’s value, which then triggers more buying to match the index. The cycle repeats, potentially pushing Bitcoin and MSTR higher indefinitely. Bitcoin to infinity? 😅 ( im ready to read comments 👀)

PS added for context : https://x.com/hillery_dan/status/1867625112737857818?s=46&t=8h8dWz0_xLtAyNlRtCFu1Q

### Post #588: 2024-12-18 22:29 `[News]`

**📉 When the Market Goes Full Tomato After FOMC: What to Do? 🍅**


1️⃣ Breathe, Don’t Panic
The market’s not broken; it’s just having a little drama. Think of it as a toddler in a candy aisle—this too shall pass.

2️⃣ Check Your Plan
Do you have a strategy, or are you just here for the vibes? If it’s the latter, maybe revisit that! Red days = discount shopping if you’ve done your homework.

3️⃣ Avoid the Herd Mentality
Everyone selling? Don’t join the stampede. Remember: “Be fearful when others are greedy, and greedy when others are fearful” (Uncle Warren said it, not me).

4️⃣ Zoom Out
One red day doesn’t define the market. Look at a 5-year chart and ask yourself, “Is this the end of the world, or just a paper cut?”

5️⃣ Invest in Snacks, Not Emotions
When in doubt, grab some popcorn and watch the chaos. Markets have short memories; this FOMC drama might be tomorrow’s forgotten headline. ( who knows )

Remember: Red days are not the apocalypse. Keep calm and let your money hustle smarter, not harder. 💪💸



*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/rkx/when-the-market-goes-full-toma*

### Post #630: 2025-01-03 01:02 — `$UBER`

**🚀 How to turn $1,300 into $100K in 17 Trades? Let’s Break It Down📈**


Here’s a theoretical path to hitting $100K with a 30% gain per trade (over 1–4 weeks per trade ). Yes, it’s ambitious—but hey, 2025 is the year for boom or gloom, right? And spreads might just be an answer. Either lose $1,300 or make it to $100k!

Let’s dive in:

🔢 The Numbers (No Pressure, Just Math):
	•	Start with $1,300.
	•	Grow it by 30% per trade.
	•	Repeat 17 times.
	•	End goal? $112,455.50.

Here’s how it compounds:

        1.	Initial Balance: $1,300
	2.	After 1st Trade: $1,300 → $1,690
	3.	After 2nd Trade: $1,690 → $2,197
	4.	After 3rd Trade: $2,197 → $2,856.10
	5.	After 4th Trade: $2,856.10 → $3,712.93
	6.	After 5th Trade: $3,712.93 → $4,826.81
	7.	After 6th Trade: $4,826.81 → $6,274.85
	8.	After 7th Trade: $6,274.85 → $8,157.31
	9.	After 8th Trade: $8,157.31 → $10,604.50
	10.	After 9th Trade: $10,604.50 → $13,785.85
	11.	After 10th Trade: $13,785.85 → $17,921.61
	12.	After 11th Trade: $17,921.61 → $23,298.10
	13.	After 12th Trade: $23,298.10 → $30,287.53
	14.	After 13th Trade: $30,287.53 → $39,373.79
	15.	After 14th Trade: $39,373.79 → $51,185.93
	16.	After 15th Trade: $51,185.93 → $66,541.71
	17.	After 16th Trade: $66,541.71 → $86,504.23
	18.	After 17th Trade: $86,504.23 → $112,455.50

😎 Why Spreads?
Spreads let you maximize potential returns if you can spot trends on a stock. Perfect for controlled growth. 

🤔 What’s the Catch?
As your account grows, placing larger trades becomes trickier. Bigger accounts need smarter strategies, more discipline, and (let’s be honest) a bit of luck.

Below is a risky but clear example of how you can achieve %30 with a spread:

Lets say you are bullish on $UBER and you believe that the stock price will end above $62 by Jan 10th.

If you do a spread of $61/62 now, it pays $24 for every $76. You can lose it all if $UBER is below $61.76 on Jan 10 at 4pm. If it closes above that, then you earn %31.5 on the initial investment!  It sounds easy in theory, but in practice it may be more difficult as you must wait for the perfect time to place the spread . You may close it before expiration and earn less then the %30 or if you believe trend went against you, also close it before you lose %100. 

The good about spreads is that you can be bullish or bearish  and you can bet on your own price target by X date. 

The bad about spreads is that if your time is not correct, you can lose %100 of the investment by expiration.

Have anyone tried spreads?

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/u36/how-to-turn-dollar1300-into-do*

### Post #638: 2025-01-04 03:26 — `$CHGG, $BIDU, $LPSN` `[News]`

**When your cash is worth more than your stock… 🤔💸**

Cash per share is a way of looking at how much cash a company has for each share of stock in circulation. Imagine you have a piggy bank full of money. If you could divide that money evenly among all the people who own a piece of the piggy bank (the stockholders), each person’s share of the cash is the cash per share.

So, next time you hear about a stock with more cash than its price, just think: Is this a hidden treasure or is the market missing something? 🤷‍♂️💸

Let’s say a company has $1 million in cash and 500,000 shares of stock:

This means that for every share you own, there’s $2 in cash backing it up.

Why is this important?

If a company’s stock price is lower than its cash per share, it could mean the stock is undervalued.

It’s like if your stock is only worth $1 per share, but the company has $2 in cash for every share. That’s a bargain, right? It’s like buying something for less than its value!

Did you know that:

$BIDU has $73.57 cash per share
$CHGG has $3.46 cash per share
$LPSN has $1.56 cash per share ( @zrosiez )

Source: Finviz

They are either a value trap or too undervalued and can 3x  or 5x very quickly on any positive news.

 And yes, there are many other variables to consider before deciding if it is a value trap or market is not correct.

Take a closer look at what the business actually does , how do they make their money? Is their yearly revenue shrinking? Are they swimming in free cash flow? Snapping up their own shares? How much debt are they carrying? There are tons of factors to consider, but the goal here is to shine a spotlight on cash per share and share some clear examples of companies that have been buzzing in After Hours—or ones I’ve personally bet my money on!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/vvw/when-your-cash-is-worth-more-t*

### Post #639: 2025-01-04 19:40 — `$SQ`

**The Rule of 40:  A “Secret” Formula for Spotting High-Performing Growth Stocks!**

Okay, imagine you have a magic number that tells you if a lemonade stand is doing great. The Rule of 40 is like that magic number for businesses! 🌟

It helps you figure out if a company is growing fast and also making good money. Here’s how it works:

Step 1: Growth 🚀

How fast is the company selling more stuff or making more money? For example, if your lemonade stand is selling 30% more lemonade than last year, that’s your growth!

Step 2: Profit 💰

Now, after buying all your lemons and sugar, how much money do you have left? If you have 15% of your lemonade sales left as profit, that’s your profit margin!

Now, let’s add them together!
So, if your lemonade stand is growing at 30% and your profit margin is 15%, you get:

30 + 15 = 45


Woohoo! That’s 45%—which is greater than 40%! 🎉 This means your stand is doing super well, growing and making money at the same time!

Why Is This Cool?

When businesses follow the Rule of 40, it means they’re growing fast and making smart choices with their money—just like how you’d want your lemonade stand to grow without wasting too much on lemons! 🍋

Did you know that $SQ is in path to hit the rule of 40 this year? ( One year earlier than anticipated?)

Any comments below 👇 are welcome 🤗 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/vAr/the-rule-of-40-a-secret-formul*

### Post #654: 2025-01-08 21:51 `[News]`

**📊 Tracking Market with Sector ETFs 📈**

Market  is like a game of musical chairs for sectors. Money moves around as economic conditions shift. Here are 12 sector-specific ETFs that can help you track and monitor sector performance in real time:

Technology Sector (XLK)
🚀 ETF: Technology Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLK)
Tracks tech companies in hardware, software, and IT services. Ideal for growth investors, especially during economic expansions.

Financial Sector (XLF)
💸 ETF: Financial Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLF)
Focuses on banks, insurance, and investment firms. Often outperforms when interest rates rise.

Healthcare Sector (XLV)
🏥 ETF: Health Care Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLV)
Includes pharmaceuticals, biotechnology, and healthcare providers. A defensive sector that performs well in diverse economic conditions.

Consumer Discretionary Sector (XLY)
🛍️ ETF: Consumer Discretionary Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLY)
Tracks companies in retail, leisure, and automobiles. Tends to perform well when consumer spending increases during growth periods.

Consumer Staples Sector (XLP)
🥫 ETF: Consumer Staples Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLP)
Focuses on companies producing essential goods like food and beverages. A go-to defensive sector during economic downturns.

Energy Sector (XLE)
⛽ ETF: Energy Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLE)
Covers companies in oil, gas, and energy production. Thrives when energy demand increases or commodity prices rise.

Industrial Sector (XLI)
🚂 ETF: Industrial Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLI)
Tracks industries like aerospace and transportation. Performs well in periods of economic growth and infrastructure spending.

Materials Sector (XLB)
🏗️ ETF: Materials Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLB)
Includes companies in raw materials and construction. Benefits from rising demand in infrastructure and industrial projects.

Real Estate Sector (XLRE)
🏢 ETF: Real Estate Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLRE)
Tracks real estate development and REITs. Sensitive to interest rate changes, making it key during fluctuating market conditions.

Utilities Sector (XLU)
⚡ ETF: Utilities Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLU)
Focuses on essential services like electricity and water. A defensive sector, often performing well during market volatility.

Communication Services Sector (XLC)
📱 ETF: Communication Services Select Sector SPDR Fund (XLC)
Includes media, telecommunications, and entertainment. Can benefit from growing trends in digital media consumption.

Materials Sector (IYM)
🏭 ETF: iShares U.S. Basic Materials ETF (IYM)
Focuses on companies in raw materials like metals and chemicals. Highly sensitive to economic cycles and global demand.

Tracking these sector ETFs can help you understand where market rotation is happening and identify sectors poised for growth or stability.  💼📊

Ideally have them in a separate ETFs watch list and see how they behave!

What do they have similar over 10 , 15 year periods ?🤔

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/wP5/tracking-market-with-sector-et*

### Post #656: 2025-01-09 04:08 `[News]`

**💡Have You Checked the “Unclaimed Property” Vault? 🏦👀**

Not about stocks, but about money 💵 

What if I told you there’s a hidden stash of money out there… and it might have your name on it? 

🔥 Enter Unclaimed Property:
Banks, utilities, and even past employers often lose track of money that’s yours…think forgotten paychecks, refunds, or old accounts. 💸

💡 How to find it:
1️⃣ Visit Unclaimed.org (official, safe, and FREE).
2️⃣ Search your name 
3️⃣ File a claim to collect your cash

### Post #659: 2025-01-09 19:12 — `$LPSN, $MQ` `[Discuss]`

**💡 Wanna Know What a Company Really Does? Here’s How to Crack the Code! 🚀**

Before diving headfirst into investing, it’s super important to understand two key things about any company:

1️⃣ What does the company actually do? (How they make their money?)
2️⃣ Who are some of their clients?

One sneaky-smart way to figure this out? 👉 Check out the “Case Studies”, “Partners” or “Customer Stories” page on their website. You’ll often find golden nuggets about their products, services, and real-world impact there! 🏆

Let’s Take Cloudflare as an Example 🌐✨
https://www.cloudflare.com/case-studies/

Cloudflare is one of my favorite companies of the future. I remember buying this when it was $35 and sold when it went to $80 the first time. 

Think about it: what business can survive without the “internet” these days? None, right? 😏

Cloudflare has significantly influenced how the internet functions, although it didn’t change the core invention of the internet itself. Instead, it enhanced how the internet operates by addressing key challenges like performance, security, and scalability. I believe what they're doing with the internet will become essential for the survival of any future company.

🎯 Check Out Cloudflare’s Rockstar Client List: (Just a brief list)

🏀 Nike
🍎 Apple
🤖 OpenAI (Yes, the creators of ChatGPT!)
🛒 Walmart & Temu
🖥️ Dell , Google Shopify
🎮 Discord & 9GAG
🌟 Canva, Etsy, Padlet
✈️ Japan Airlines

And that’s just scratching the surface!


Some other interesting case studies from other AH tickers:

$LPSN  https://www.liveperson.com/resources/customers/

$MQ  https://www.marqeta.com/resources?resources%5BrefinementList%5D%5Btype%5D%5B0%5D=Case%20study

Which company are you curious about? Did you learn anything?

### Post #660: 2025-01-10 02:31 — `$UBER, $SQ, $DASH, $COIN, $MQ` `[Dd]`

**Understanding $MQ**

Anyone here knows what ramp is? https://ramp.com
I cant think of any other way how medium to large size companies can handle corporate credit card purchases without a tool like this… ( They are $MQ 🤫 client)

$UBER $DASH $COIN and $SQ are also client🤔

Alright going back to Marqeta, imagine Marqeta as the “cool tech wizard” of payment cards!

They help businesses create custom cards you can use to pay for stuff, like the ones you swipe or tap at stores. But these aren’t just regular cards, they’re smart cards.

Here’s what Marqeta does: ( most people don’t understand what they do)

1- Companies can make their own cards, kind of like designing their own merch. These cards can be physical (you hold them) or virtual (used online).

2-Businesses can decide where and how much money the card can spend. For example, a delivery company might say, “This card only works for buying gas and food!”

3-Say you’re an Uber driver, and you want your money right now, Marqeta helps make that happen instantly.

4-They have tech to stop hackers and scammers from using your card.


Basically, Marqeta is like a backstage crew making sure payments work smoothly for apps like DoorDash, Uber, or your favorite delivery service. They keep everything fast, safe, and super customizable.

Isn’t this interesting?



*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/wZT/understanding-dollarmq*
