### Post #672: 2025-01-13 23:34 `[News]`

**Overtrading: Don’t Turn 12 Months into 120 Trades**


Overtrading is like celebrating all 12 months of the year in a single week….chaos, burnout, and way too much cake (or losses). Each month has its purpose, just like each trade should. Imagine throwing a Halloween party in July or setting off fireworks in March…it doesn’t make sense, right?

So, unless you’re trying to break the world record for most trades in a day or a year, take it easy! Overtrading burns money, sanity, and your keyboard. Stick to your plan, wait for the right setups, and remember, there’s no prize for being the busiest trader, just the smartest one.

Because in trading, just like in life, sometimes the best move… is no move at all.🫶


*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/xyY/overtrading-dont-turn-12-month*

### Post #681: 2025-01-15 16:13 `[Discuss]`

**🎭 Drama in the Options Market: BABA $100 Calls March 2025 🍿**

Yesterday, the volume for BABA’s $100 call expiring March 21 was 15,046 contracts traded, with an open interest of 39,299. But today? We’ve got a spike in open interest at 49,463! 📈 Meanwhile, volume has cooled off to 1,026.

So, what does this mean? 🤔

Volume: The number of contracts traded during the day. Yesterday’s high volume means a lot of activity, either people opening or closing contracts.

Open Interest: The total number of contracts still open (not yet closed or expired). The jump in open interest today shows many traders opened new positions yesterday! 💼

Are traders bullish? 🤔 The increased open interest suggests commitment, and with a strike price of $100 (currently out of the money), buyers might be betting BABA will rally hard! 🚀

Or are sellers being cheeky? They might be selling these calls, predicting BABA won’t make the $100 mark by March. 🐻

💡 Takeaway: Volume is like gossip at a party, but open interest is who sticks around for the after-party. Watch both to spot market sentiment!

🫶


*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/xSH/drama-in-the-options-market-ba*

### Post #683: 2025-01-16 01:58

**To succeed in life💪**

Life will knock you down…..it’s practically in the job description. But here’s the secret: the win isn’t about never falling, it’s about always getting back up. 🥊

Think of it like the stock market:

📉 Bad day? Stock ltanks 20%.
📈 The next day? Rally mode ON.

Your life’s the same. Failures? They’re just temporary dips in your chart. Every time you stand back up, you’re building momentum for the next breakout! 🚀

💡 The Lesson:
Falling down doesn’t make you a failure. Staying down does. So, dust off, learn, adjust your strategy, and come back stronger.

Whether you’re climbing the career ladder, growing your portfolio, or chasing your dreams…remember: The only way to lose is to stop showing up.🫶

### Post #721: 2025-01-25 02:37 `[News]`

**🚀 Meet DeepSeek: The $5.6M AI Underdog Shaking Up Silicon Valley (and Your Portfolio!)🚀**


Hey finance fam! Let’s talk about  “DeepSeek” the Chinese AI startup that’s flipping the script on Big Tech and geopolitics. Think “David vs. Goliath,” but with more GPUs and fewer slingshots. 😎  

What’s DeepSeek?

A scrappy AI lab from Hangzhou, backed by a quant hedge fund , that’s building  open-source AI models rivaling OpenAI and Google but at a fraction of the cost. Their secret sauce? Innovation on a budget🤑.  

Why Markets Should Care💼  

AI Democratization: Small firms can now compete with Big Tech. DeepSeek’s models run on laptops, no $1B supercomputers needed .  

DeepSeek claimed it used just 2,048 Nvidia H800s and $5.6mn to train a model with 671bn parameters, a fraction of what OpenAI and Google spent to train comparably sized models.

China had a much larger talent pool of systems engineers than the US who understand how to get the best use of computing resources to train and run models more cheaply.

China’s AI Surge: Sanctions backfired! China’s AI sector is innovating faster, threatening U.S. dominance. DeepSeek’s rise hints at a seismic shift in global tech power .  

Energy Efficiency: Their models use less power.

P.S. If OpenAI is the Tesla of AI, DeepSeek might just be the BYD. 🚘⚡   

Read more: https://www.forbes.com/sites/craigsmith/2025/01/22/deepseek-how-chinas-ai-innovators-are-challenging-the-status-quo/

### Post #723: 2025-01-26 19:16 `[News]`

**Top Productivity app 📊 Updated Today**

@Soc was paying attention and noticed that DeepSeek climbed to #2. 

Im going to be clear, I dont personally think this is a threat to US ai or NVIDIA or Microsoft etc. However, I do believe this creates competition.

What does it mean?

1- Deepseek is free and an d the end user can upload a “unlimited” attachments, while the parent ( im going to be creative here and call ChatGPT, the 👨🏻 father 😂). Will charge you after a limit of queries/ file attachments per day

2- Deepseek charges a lot less to Developers that use their API . While this is not relevant for regular/end user, it is for new developers or existing that want to save money for the same or equivalent results.

I believe these two clear examples offer benefits to the end/ regular user. 

Will it dethrone ChatGPT? No, very unlikely as it will only create competition and OpenAI will be forced to offer more for less.

Happy to hear and listen to your thoughts and arguments 🫶🦻

**🦢 Meet Goose: The AI Helper from @XYZ**


Block announced Goose, a cool new tool that lets smart AI systems (called large language models or LLMs) do real-world tasks. It’s like giving your computer a brain to help out with stuff!

Here’s how Goose works and why it’s an awesome concept :

Connects to Real Tools: Goose can plug into things like coding apps or even business tools to help people get work done faster.

Learns and Grows: Developers can add new features, so Goose keeps getting smarter. It might help with writing songs, online shopping, or maybe even creating art .

Customizable: Want Goose to look or act a certain way? Developers can make it happen.

To me it looks closer and closer how 1 person can do the job of 5 people thanks to AI.

### Post #753: 2025-01-30 16:37 — `$NVDA` `[Dd]`

**China DD**

Ok gents, I had to quickly write some of my thoughts based on recent developments.

If China cracked the code on making Large Language Models (LLMs) ultra-efficient, both in terms of power consumption and hardware dependency, what would that mean?

Maybe Lower power AI,  cheaper AI. Maybe the rest of the world ( not talking about the US,) would want to adopt it . I know China has many partnerships worldwide (https://orcasia.org/allfiles/China's_Partnerships.pdf) .. and many  of these small countries would want to pay less and receive more..They really do not care about what china is going to do with their data, blah blah blah...is not like if they are a world superpower.

Maybe a power-efficient LLM would be a game-changer for China’s energy strategy, making it less reliant on foreign chips and reducing strain on its power grid?

Imagine China licensing super-efficient AI models worldwide. Countries and companies looking to cut costs would be all over it. ( Yes I know, We have a world of superpower countries that would prefer the United States, and it would make sense, but wouldn;t this change sentiment on China stocks overall?

Maybet major shifts in market leadership. Tech giants worldwide would scramble to keep up. (not saying $NVDA will crash or whatever, but maybe big players that have been hammered for no reason will re-gain a lot of market share?


If this tech exists, wouldn't Chinese AI players (Baidu, Alibaba, Tencent) make big moves? If they go all-in, the market will follow.

Stay safe and grounded 🫶

### Post #768: 2025-02-02 21:53

**Why I’m Not Afraid of Trump’s Tariffs**

Look, I’ve seen some things. I’ve watched the markets throw full-blown tantrums, and guess what? I’m still here. So, when people start panicking about Trump’s tariffs and what they might do to stocks, I just sip my coffee and remember what I have survived.

2018 Trade War:
Trump’s first round of tariffs hit, and the market got whiplash. Everyone freaked out. I stayed put. The S&P dropped 20%, but did it recover? 

March 16, 2020 :The Fastest Crash in History
COVID panic wiped out a third of the market in weeks. People were selling stocks like they were cursed objects. I held on. Within months, we were hitting new highs.

2022 :The Slow, Painful Bear Market
Inflation, rate hikes, and doom-filled headlines. Stocks bled for months. But if you stuck around, you saw a bull market return in 2023.

So, Tariffs? Please.

Markets don’t die from headlines, they adapt. Companies adjust, supply chains shift, and investors who stay calm win in the long run.

Sure, there might be volatility, but I’ve danced with worse. And if history has taught me anything, it’s that patience pays, panic doesn’t.

🫶

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/BbA/why-im-not-afraid-of-trumps-ta*

### Post #792: 2025-02-08 00:41 `[Discuss]`

**📈 Trading Without a Framework?**

If you’re making trades based on gut feelings and hot tips, you’re basically gambling. 🎲

A solid trading/investing framework keeps you:
✅ Focused on your strategy (day trade, swing, or long-term)
✅ Protected with risk management 
✅ Confident with analysis (charts, fundamentals, or both)
✅ Disciplined in execution (know when to enter & exit)
✅ Emotionally strong, because the market LOVES to test your patience 😤

Stick to a framework, or the market will make you its lesson. 📉😂

That’s why I created www.Wbfintool.com ,  to empower AfterHour users, ignite inspiration, and spark the idea of creating a strong trading and investing framework! 🚀📈

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/Cnp/trading-without-a-framework*

### Post #797: 2025-02-09 13:10 — `$MSTR` `[Discuss]`

**Poor Man’s Covered Call: The Budget-Friendly Way to Sell Options! ( With Real example)📝**


Want to make money selling calls but don’t have the cash for 100 shares? No worries, there’s a cheaper way,the Poor Man’s Covered Call (PMCC) or Synthetic Covered Call.

How It Works (Super Simple!)

Buy a long-term call (LEAPS) : This is your “discounted” stock that gives you the right to buy shares later.

 Sell a short-term call : This is how you collect premium $$$, just like a regular covered call!

Why It’s Cool

✅ Way cheaper than buying 100 shares
✅ Generates passive income from selling calls
✅ Uses leverage smartly (but don’t go crazy!)

Real Example

$MSTR  is $329.20 Buying 100 shares costs $32,920! Instead, you:

✔ Buy ITM LEAPS call (expires next year or near a year depending your budget ) $300 strike costs around $11,600 - This is way less than $32,920 to control 100 shares 🧠

Of course , you start a PMCC when stock has been down + IV down🤷🏻‍♂️

✔ Sell a short-term call and collect $1,200 every few weeks? ( if things go on your favor ) Maybe the $345 strike price on Feb 21?

Boom! You’re running a covered call strategy without needing a fortune.

Risk? If the stock moons 🚀 past your strike price, you cap your gains. But hey, that’s the trade-off for spending way less upfront!

Ever tried the Poor Man’s Covered Call? ☎️ 

PS: This is not financial advice. Always do your own research and consult your financial advisor or broker to understand the risks involved.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/Cr9/poor-mans-covered-call-the-bud*

**🚨Big Day Tomorrow for Chinese Stock🚨**

Tomorrow is going to be a pivotal day for Chinese stocks, and here’s why. 📈 Michael Burry has been signaling for a while now that Chinese stocks are seriously undervalued ,  we're talking a potential 50% discount! 💸 And guess what? The 13F filing is dropping tomorrow, which means we'll get to see if Burry has been making moves in China.

I’ve been in the China market for a while now,  even before Charlie Munger jumped in at around $200 per share on Alibaba (and for the record, the share count was higher back then 😉). It’s been a long journey for me as I have been waiting for the perfect catalysts and cycle to kick in...I have a feeling that the cycle is finally turning, and we’re about to see good Chinese stocks move to where they truly belong. 🚀

Now, let’s talk possible scenarios for tomorrow’s big reveal:

✅Burry discloses no change in holdings: China stocks will likely continue the uptrend 🚀

✅Burry reveals he bought more during the November dip: Expect the rocket to soar even higher 🚀🚀

🩸 Burry closes his positions: Stocks might take a little breather 🛑

There’s a 66.66% chance Chinese stocks are heading higher tomorrow, and a 33.34% chance we’ll see a breather. 🤞 I’m hoping for the first one.

Stay tuned and let’s see what happens tomorrow! 💥📈

Michael Burry's 13F  Q4 2024 filings, which disclose the holdings of his investment firm, Scion Asset Management, is due by tomorrow.

The 13F filing deadlines are as follow for anyone interested:

Q1 (January-March): Due by May 15
Q2 (April-June): Due by August 14
Q3 (July-September): Due by November 14
Q4 (October-December): Due by February 14

PS: Save the “Burry predicted the crash 5000th times” comments….we've heard it a thousand times! 😅 Instead, focus on studying the investors . It’ll have a far bigger impact on your financial future. 💡📚

### Post #847: 2025-02-14 21:48 — `$ETSY` `[Contest]`

**$ETSY Spread closed by Robinhood**

If you let an in-the-money (ITM) option expire without any risk from Robinhood, they will automatically buy or sell the shares at closing based on your selected strike price (see attached photos). However, if the option is at risk, meaning it’s too close to the strike price and they might not be able to sell the call…they may choose to close it automatically around 3:00 PM or 3:30 PM.

The difference between the spread is the profit.🫶

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/DAd/dollaretsy-spread-closed-by-ro*

### Post #852: 2025-02-16 18:17

**Why Blindly Copying Trades Is Dumb**

Copying trades without understanding them is like trying to bake a cake without knowing the recipe… ( Unless you copy  Pelossi 😂)

Jokes aside, every trader has their own plan, risk level, and reason for buying or selling. If you just follow along without knowing why, you won’t know what to do when things go wrong .

Even I don’t want you to blindly copy my trades..No one wins all the time, and markets change fast. Instead of just copying, learn why a trade makes sense. That way, you can make smarter decisions and not just hope for the best. Because let’s be real, hope is not a strategy. 🫶

I measure all trades based on probability from %0 to %100 and understand that this changes day by day.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/DJ1/why-blindly-copying-trades-is-*

### Post #853: 2025-02-16 20:12 `[Discuss]`

**Tired of TradingView’s Free Version Limits? 🔥**

If you’re using the free version of TradingView, you know it only lets you use two indicators. 😬 That can feel pretty restricting when you’re trying to dive deeper into the markets and want to use TradingView but not paying more for extra indicators .

But don’t worry, we've got you covered! On our TradingView Tools page at wbfintool.com/trading-view, we give you access to five powerful  indicators in tradingview  that can complement your charting experience: ( I like to quickly jump in a chart without having to login to anything and check cool stuff. PC view will give you more screen of you are really serious about it 😅)

Below are the indicators I included and I think may offer valuable information.

💎Bollinger Bands: Tracks market volatility. The bands widen when the market is volatile and narrow when it’s stable.

💎Historical Volatility (HV): Measures how much the price of an asset has fluctuated over a specific period.

💎Ichimoku Cloud: A comprehensive tool for spotting support/resistance levels, momentum, and trend direction.

💎Momentum (Mom): Measures the speed of price movements.

💎RSI (Relative Strength Index): Identifies overbought or oversold conditions in an asset.

PS: Charting platforms are like pizza toppings, everyone has their own favorite! Some prefer TradingView, others swear by something else (like Webull, Thinkorswim or even Robinhood Legend), and that’s totally cool. They all offer great features for free.

Technical analysis isn’t magic. It’s not going to make or break your trade on its own, but it can definitely sprinkle in some extra data to help you make a more informed decision.￼

Feel free to swing by www.wbfintool.com and check out some seriously useful tools! At first, I created this for my own use, but then I thought, “Why not share the love with AfterHour?” It might just have a positive impact on others too. 🌟

What indicators you like and why?

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/DKx/tired-of-tradingviews-free-ver*

### Post #951: 2025-03-04 21:46 `[News]`

**Stay cool or stay out ! ❤️📧 ❤️**

I post a lot…when the market is redder than a crime scene and when it’s as green as a giant… well, you get the idea. I don’t post to impress anyone, just to share my thoughts. If one or two people find value in it, that’s good enough for me!

So, if you’re here to drop negative comments or add zero value…save yourself the effort. Different opinions? Cool. Just keep it respectful.

Earlier, when the market rallied out of nowhere, I shared how my portfolio handled it. Did it stress me out? Nope. I know my risk, I have a plan, and patience is my superpower. But one user thought I was claiming to predict the bounce and got all worked up in a negative way…. My response? A quick wave of my magic wand, and poof, problem solved. 🪄 

For those just starting out: If market swings make you nervous, you’re either overleveraged or too deep in the game. Be smart about risk. I have no clue what tomorrow brings…bloodbath or rally….but I do know I’m comfortable with the risk I take.

I don’t day trade, I don’t try to predict intraday moves. But when a company I like hits a market cap that makes sense to me? I buy. Simple.

Stay safe, trade smart, and don’t let the noise shake you. ❤️

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/GCZ/stay-cool-or-stay-out*

### Post #975: 2025-03-07 08:30 — `$BIDU, $BABA` `[News]`

**Introducing The New Stock Price Calculator!**


Big news! I just dropped a game-changing tool, the New Stock Projections Calculator. Built to help you automatically see what the market is pricing in for your favorite stocks. 📊🔍This tool is designed to give you a serious edge in the market by unlocking insights into stock price movements and implied volatility.

🔍 Key Benefits on the new version:

🔥Automatic Data Pull: Instantly see stock prices and implied volatility

🔥Cool Company Logos: Search a stock and get a sleek logo displayed, because who doesn’t love a visual vibe?

🔥Personalized Projections: Think you’re smarter than Mr. Market? Tweak the values (like implied volatility) to create custom projections that match your gut.

 🔥Share on X: Show off your genius! Share your projections directly to X with a photo attachment in just a click.

Heads Up: For penny stocks / low-liquidity names  or without options contracts, implied volatility won’t auto populate. No worries, you can manually adjust it based on your own research, making this tool super flexible!

📊 What’s the Stock Projections Calculator?

It’s your new go-to for projecting future stock prices using real-time market data and volatility. Whether you’re hunting undervalued gems or spotting trends, this tool helps you see what Mr Market is  thinking, so you can act smarter. This is the first calculator of its kind as it allows you to adjust the values and create your own projections based on Implied Volatility and stock price. This calculator is inspired by the Black-Scholes model. What makes it different is that it is easier to understand, customizable, and vibrant UI. ( Finance apps or brokerages that have these studies really suck at UI)  

🔗 Try it now and let me know what you think! Share your projections on X!
https://wbfintool.com/stock-price-calculator

💡 How Can You Use It?

Here are a couple of real-world examples to get you started:

💎Value Investing Play:
Imagine you’re eyeing $BIDU at $90 and think it’s a steal because you analyzed the company and market cap + growth looks too cheap to pass. Plug it into the calculator, and you see Mr. Market’s pricing in a drop to $79. You’d happily buy it at $80, though! So, sell puts at that strike, pocket premiums while you wait for your entry. Smart, right?

💎Trend-Spotting Win:
 Let’s say $BABA’s at $84, implied volatility (IV) is crazy low, and you spot a bullish chart trend or even better, you think $BABA’s valuation is cheap. Then you notice call option volume spiking…open interest and trades are through the roof! The market’s not betting on $BABA $108 by 2026, but you see the setup. Use the calculator to project prices and jump in. (Fun fact: I did this and scored a 500% return on the $50K in the bag!) See the full verified post/thread here: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/CGR/compilation-of-my-chyna-dollar

Pro Tip:
On its own, this tool is cool, but pair it with your knowledge and other resources, and it’s a game-changer. It’s all about seeing beyond the stock price to what Mr. Market’s hinting at, then making your move when the time’s right.

Any bugs, please report to: warrenbuffett@wbfintool.com


In 2025, my mission is to create a bunch of free tools that I wish existed when I first started out….🫶

Any feedback or questions, feel free to drop them below 🫶

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/G0c/introducing-the-new-stock-pric*

### Post #1046: 2025-03-18 04:29 — `$BIDU, $BABA` `[Discuss]`

**Stock Calculator FAQ**

I’ve been asked this many times… Hopefully, the examples below will give you some ideas or inspire ways to use it to your advantage! (this is not a get rich quick tool… but learn quick )

💡 How Can You Use It?

Here are a couple of real-world examples on how I used it and to help you get started:

💎Value Investing Play:
Imagine you’re eyeing $BIDU at $90 and think it’s a steal because you analyzed the company and market cap + growth looks too cheap to pass. Plug it into the calculator, and you see Mr. Market’s pricing in a drop to $79. You’d happily buy it at $80, though! So, sell puts at that strike, pocket premiums while you wait for your entry. Smart, right? Maybe a long call is smarter 🫶

💎Trend-Spotting Win:
 Let’s say $BABA’s at $84, implied volatility (IV) is crazy low, and you spot a bullish chart trend or even better, you think $BABA’s valuation is cheap. Then you notice call option volume spiking…open interest and trades are through the roof! The market’s not betting on $BABA $108 by 2026, but you see the setup. Use the calculator to project prices and jump in. (Fun fact: I did this and scored a 500% return on the $50K in the bag!) See the full verified post/thread here: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/CGR/compilation-of-my-chyna-dollar

Pro Tip:
On its own, this tool is cool, but pair it with your knowledge and other resources, and it’s a game-changer. It’s all about seeing beyond the stock price to what Mr. Market’s hinting at, then making your move when the time’s right.


🔗 Try it now and let me know what you think! Share your projections on X!
https://wbfintool.com/stock-price-calculator


*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/Ifi/stock-calculator-faq*

### Post #1153: 2025-04-02 21:53 `[News]`

**The Best AH Tool Available Now! 🚀**

Hey, AH Fam!  Today was a rough day…. But hopefully brighter days  will be there for us! 

Anyways! Ive  been working on something that some of you may find  very useful ! (especially in this zig/zag market !) Tired of guessing your way through the stock market jungle? Want to trade a little bit smarter? Say hello to the DCA (all in one) Calculator. Your new best friend! This isn’t just another tool....it’s your ticket to navigate the markets with more confidence. Whether you’re a Wall Street wannabe or a seasoned stock slinger, this calculator’s got your back with three game-changing features that’ll have you trading like a pro.


🎢 Dollar-Cost Averaging: 

Ever bought a stock at the peak, only to watch it crash and burn? Ouch! With the DCA mode, you can kiss those panic attacks goodbye. This feature lets you punch in your past buys/shares, prices and spits out your average cost if you plan to add at current prices...with profit/loss, and how far away in % you are from the current price. It’s like having a crystal ball that says, “Chill, you’ve got this!” Spread your investments over time, smooth out those wild market swings, and build your portfolio like a boss. More consistency, less stress...DCA may be your secret weapon for steady gains!

Trading hint: Lower your risk and sleep better at night knowing you’re not betting it all on one crazy day.


🎯 Target Average Price: Hit Your Sweet Spot!

Got a stock you love but paid too much? Dreaming of a lower average price to brag about? The Target Avg Price mode is here to make it happen! Plug in your current shares, what you paid, and your dream average price, and—BOOM—this calculator tells you exactly how many shares to snag at today’s price to hit that target. It’s like a treasure map to your perfect position, showing you the capital you’ll need to get there. Precision? Check. Profit potential? Double check!


💰 Deploy Bankroll: Make Every Dollar Count!

Got a chunk of cash burning a hole in your pocket? The Bankroll mode is your personal money-deploying machine! Tell it how much you’ve got to invest, date you want to be fully invested, and any existing shares you’re holding. This bad boy calculates a daily investment plan, estimates your total shares, and estimates your average cost—all while factoring in fancy stuff like implied volatility. It’s like having a financial wizard whispering, “Here’s how to stretch your dollars for max impact.” This calculator factors in how much downside Mr Market is pricing in so you can spread out the cost if you believe the stock is in a downtrend, zig-zag, but believe it will be up  the date after you deploy all our bags.

Trading hint: Turn your cash pile into a strategic powerhouse, timing your buys to dominate the market over days or weeks or months.

Any questions? include them below!

Try it now and let me know🫶

https://dca.wbfintool.com

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/JDp/the-best-ah-tool-available-now*

### Post #1172: 2025-04-05 03:43 — `$SPY` `[News]`

**BEAR MARKET SURVIVAL GUIDE 📖**

Ah, the bear market… where your portfolio looks like a crime scene, and your coffee consumption hits all-time highs. But don’t panic! Stay mentally strong! Here’s how to ride the storm like a pro and come out stronger:

🐻 DON’T FIGHT THE BEAR : Accept that markets go up AND down. It’s not fun, but it’s normal. Take a deep breath and zoom out…. every bear market in history has eventually led to a bull market. EVERY.

💰 CASH IS KING (SOMETIMES) : If you’re freaking out, having some cash on the side can help you buy stocks at a discount. Make sure you only hold companies that you believe in…else is better to not hold any until you are mentally prepared. 

📉 DCA LIKE A CHAMP : Instead of trying to “buy the dip” (which somehow keeps dipping), and can drain you mentally, use Dollar-Cost Averaging (DCA) to invest gradually over time. https://dca.wbfintool.com No stress, just strategy without thinking that you have to sell , because at the end, if you keep buying $SPY for 12 months, for example, it is almost certain that you will not be in a bad position. Plan ahead..

🚀 QUALITY OVER HYPE : Meme stocks and hype plays might not survive, but strong, profitable companies usually do. Stick to the real MVPs. @DocHollywood a lot about those.

⏳ REMEMBER: THIS TOO SHALL PASS : Every bear market (downtrend) in history has led to a bull market. Stay patient and future-you will thank you.

📖LEARN & ADAPT: Use this time to study past bear markets, improve your investing strategy, and sharpen your risk management skills….

Thanks to the internet and social media, news spreads faster than ever. This means markets can tank in seconds over bad news or skyrocket just as fast on good news. Gone are the days when trends took their sweet time to form; now, one tweet can send stocks on a rollercoaster ride.

Bear markets test investors, but they also build wealth for those who stay disciplined. Play the long game, and you’ll come out stronger on the other side… do not YOLO😅

We’re all in this together…similar goals, similar struggles, and hopefully, way more memes to get us through it. Losing money? Yeah, it sucks. But learning to handle it like a champ and moving forward? That’s what separates the pros from the novice. Stay positive, take it one day at a time, and remember: green days always come back… eventually. 💚📈😂

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/J0S/bear-market-survival-guide*

### Post #1175: 2025-04-05 13:14 — `$AAPL` `[Dd]`

**Trying  to Understand Exports  and How Affects Global Markets 🫶**

You don’t need a PhD to understand what’s happening…you just need to look at the data and use a little common sense.

Check this out:
https://tradingeconomics.com/united-states/exports-by-country

It’s crystal clear: the U.S. is way more dependent on Asia than China is on the U.S. Go ahead, read that again.

This is based on 2023 data, and I’m 100% sure China’s reliance on the U.S. has dropped even more since then. And here’s a fun thought…if you’re a country anywhere in the Americas, who are you more likely to trade with? Probably not the one trying to break up with you economically.

Now, let’s connect some dots. Remember when the real Warren Buffett started hoarding cash… and even sold a piece of his Apple stock? It starts to make a lot more sense when you realize what China’s shipping over here….

Their top export to the U.S.? Telephony equipment. Yeah…think iPhones. Think $AAPL.
(And probably the only continent that can ship these anyways🤷🏻‍♂️ ) India?🇮🇳  🇻🇳 Vietnam,  South Korea 🇰🇷, Hong Kong 🇭🇰?..Maybe you need to take Geography again.. and yes… US is about %80 dependent on Asia for telephony… and Warren knew it. Get ready to buy your iPhone at $10,000😅

### Post #1216: 2025-04-10 15:17 `[News]`

**Investing in a Changing World 🌎  by Howard Marks**


https://wbfintool.com/the-rules-are-changing-10-principles-for-investing-in-an-uncertain-world-by-howard-marks/

1- The world is not ending. But the rules are changing

2-Countries are re-thinking trade

3-This matter because trade is not just a political issue, but an economic engine.

4-Rethink the context for every investing decision

5- Do not rely on old models in a new world

6-Forecasting does not work in enviroments like this. It is not a flaw, it is a reality

7- Make probability, positioning your best friend

8-When uncertainty is high, discipline matters more.

9-Don’t confuse falling prices with rising risks. Markets go on sale. That is part of the cycle. The critical question is not “Will it go lowes?” it is “Am I being compensated fairly for the risk Im taking?”

10- US still remains a strong place to invest.

### Post #1228: 2025-04-12 00:12 `[News]`

**Shifting Supply Chains: A Glimpse into Global Manufacturing Changes**

Hey everyone! I wanted to share a quick true story. Last week, I reached out to a major computer manufacturer about placing a large order for customized computer devices. Today, they confirmed that my order will be built in Vietnam instead of China, where their production typically takes place. The reason? They’re moving operations out of China to avoid tariffs. In fact, they’ve officially stopped production there.

For at least the next 90 days, all manufacturing will be done in Vietnam, in line with current policies. Depending on the volume of the order, there could be some delays, but they’re doing their best to manage the transition smoothly.

It’s just another reminder of how rapidly the global landscape is shifting…economics, trade, and production are all evolving, and companies are trying and “adapting”fast. 

Will this affect estimates or projections? Is this good or bad for US in the next Qs? Did you know that all computer manufacturers outsource their production in Asia? Only some specialized / government are assembled or manufactured in US.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/KVQ/shifting-supply-chains-a-glimp*

### Post #1256: 2025-04-15 22:52 — `$NVDA, $BIDU` `[News]`

**Thesis is shaping up 🇨🇳**

Well, the AI war is just starting… $NVDA chips banned in china, and I don’t think is necessarily a bad thing.

I do believe one company that no one is talking will benefit from this $BIDU . They don’t need US and they don’t need $NVDA chips… The world is big enough . 

Top 3 AI Chips in China:

Huawei Ascend 910B: Likely the most sold in China due to its broad adoption by major tech firms (e.g., Baidu’s 2023 order of 1,600 units for 200 servers) and Huawei’s push for domestic self-sufficiency. Its integration into cloud services and government-backed projects gives it an edge.

Baidu Kunlun II: Significant deployment in Baidu’s data centers, but its use is more specialized within Baidu’s ecosystem, making it less widespread than Ascend.

Biren BR100: Less widely sold due to production constraints and a less mature software stack, limiting its adoption compared to Huawei’s offering.
Most Sold Worldwide (from these three)

None of these chips are major players globally due to U.S. sanctions and China’s focus on domestic markets. They are not exported at scale, as China prioritizes meeting internal AI demand. Huawei’s Ascend 910B has the most potential for limited international use (e.g., in allied nations), but no data confirms significant global sales. Worldwide, Nvidia’s H100 and A100 dominate AI chip sales.

One thing to note is that China has a better electricity backbone / infrastructure and double the US in generation capacity .. isn’t this needed to be able to run and sell affordable AI? I do think that Donny is pushing things that will benefit China at the end.



*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/LdP/thesis-is-shaping-up*

### Post #1299: 2025-04-23 23:50

**Dollar Cost Averaging  Tools**

dca.wbfintool.com

🎢 Dollar-Cost Averaging: 

Ever bought a stock at the peak, only to watch it crash and burn? Ouch! With the DCA mode, you can kiss those panic attacks goodbye. This feature lets you punch in your past buys/shares, prices and spits out your average cost if you plan to add at current prices...with profit/loss, and how far away in % you are from the current price. It’s like having a crystal ball that says, “Chill, you’ve got this!” Spread your investments over time, smooth out those wild market swings, and build your portfolio like a boss. More consistency, less stress...DCA may be your secret weapon for steady gains!

Trading hint: Lower your risk and sleep better at night knowing you’re not betting it all on one crazy day.

🎯 Target Average Price: Hit Your Sweet Spot!

Got a stock you love but paid too much? Dreaming of a lower average price to brag about? The Target Avg Price mode is here to make it happen! Plug in your current shares, what you paid, and your dream average price, and BOOM this calculator tells you exactly how many shares to snag at today’s price to hit that target. It’s like a treasure map to your perfect position, showing you the capital you’ll need to get there. Precision? Check. Profit potential? Double check!

💰 Deploy Bankroll: Make Every Dollar Count!

Got a chunk of cash burning a hole in your pocket? The Bankroll mode is your personal money-deploying machine! Tell it how much you’ve got to invest, date you want to be fully invested, and any existing shares you’re holding. This bad boy calculates a daily investment plan, estimates your total shares, and estimates your average cost all while factoring in fancy stuff like implied volatility. It’s like having a financial wizard whispering, “Here’s how to stretch your dollars for max impact.” This calculator factors in how much downside Mr Market is pricing in so you can spread out the cost if you believe the stock is in a downtrend, zig-zag, but believe it will be up  the date after you deploy all our bags.

Trading hint: Turn your cash pile into a strategic powerhouse, timing your buys to dominate the market over days or weeks or months.

Try it now!


*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/LSu/dollar-cost-averaging-tools*

### Post #1353: 2025-05-03 00:27 `[Discuss]`

**Reflecting on Afterhour**

Waking up that day to hundreds of notifications felt surreal .. it took a moment to fully process what was happening. Now that some time has passed and things have settled, I wanted to take a moment to share a few thoughts.

I’ve been part of Afterhour since day one. While I never had any direct connection with Kevin, I always appreciated the concept behind the platform. Through Afterhour, I’ve had the chance to connect with some of the most talented traders like @longwashere, @Climbthatladder, and @wTF …. people whose insights I continue to learn from regularly. Without AH, those connections likely wouldn’t have happened, and for that, I’m genuinely grateful.

Despite the recent controversy , which, in my opinion, could and should have been avoided…it’s important to acknowledge the many good things the app brought to the table. It created a space where investors could come together, learn, grow, and support one another. That’s something special.

That said, if the goal is to build a community focused on helping people succeed financially, launching a low-quality token and allowing users to buy it with no clear value proposition is the wrong move. There are still so many great features and essential improvements to focus on , like fixing the options sync issue, which directly affects transparency.. Getting these fundamentals right should be the top priority.

On a personal note, Afterhour has given me a platform to share both my wins and losses, along with my thought processes … in the hope that it might help others find their own path to success. It also encouraged me to share my personal project ( Never intended to share), wbfintool.com , a set of tools I use or I’ve built and continue to refine for those genuinely interested in learning. I firmly believe there's a gap between those who truly understand the markets and those who risk everything on 0dte. Helping bridging that gap has always been a core motivation for me.

At the end of the day, we’re all here for the same reasons:

1-To learn

2-To connect with smart, like-minded people

3-To build wealth


Regardless of what happens next, I’ll keep posting content that I believe adds value, and I’ll always be around on X ( See my bio)  if you ever want to connect.

Let’s take the lessons, stay focused, and keep doing what we came here to do: grow, learn, and make that money.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/MLd/reflecting-on-afterhour*

### Post #1586: 2025-07-18 23:11 — `$HOOD` `[Discuss]`

**📉 Stop Copying Trades Like It’s Homework 📈**

I saw @Longwashere post and @SIRJACK  earlier … Got inspired a bit  and wanted share this thought …

Just because someone bought $HOOD calls or $CLBR doesn’t mean you should too. 

Yes, you can be in the same stock… But you’re NOT in the same trade.

👉 The market isn’t a group project , we don’t all pass just because we’re holding the same ticker.

You can follow, learn, and even be inspired…
But blindly copying trades is how you lose faith in the game and let emotions get over you.

📌 Know YOUR why. Know YOUR plan.

💡Because the only trade that matters…
is the one you actually understand. 
❤️

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/TUf/stop-copying-trades-like-its-h*

### Post #1644: 2025-08-25 23:04 — `$PLTR, $UNH, $BTC` `[Discuss]`

**Leverage at All-Time Highs Amid Rate Cut Buzz …Is This the Top?**

Margin debt for buying stocks just hit a fresh ATH at $1.022 trillion in July – that’s over a trillion bucks of borrowed money fueling this rally. ￼ 

With the Fed signaling potential rate cuts as soon as September, lower borrowing costs could ironically push margin even higher, supercharging risk in an already frothy market. 😬

Sure, it screams “top” with so many records being shattered, but don’t write off the bull yet. Valuations aren’t insane across the board …legends like Warren Buffett initiated a new ~$1.6B position in $UNH (over 5M shares), betting on healthcare stability. ￼ 

Even contrarian Michael Burry is holding longs, with his top bet on Estée Lauder (21% of Scion’s portfolio) amid his value hunts. ￼

On crypto: $BTC blasted to an ATH earlier this month but is pausing around $110K now. Will it dip further, or could BTC ETFs keep pumping as rates drop, drawing in more institutional cash? 

Lower rates historically correlate with BTC gains, and with the halving echo still fresh, ETF inflows might override any pullback. ￼

Meanwhile, overhypers like $PLTR are cracking down 17% recently from highs, with analysts warning of a 70% plunge due to sky-high valuations not matching growth. ￼ 

Looks like rotation in play: Capital fleeing froth to rate-beaten sectors like financials, utilities, or real estate, which could rebound hard on cuts.

My take: Stay vigilant – leverage + euphoria = volatility ahead, but selective picks (think value plays à la Buffett/Burry) and BTC’s macro tailwinds could shine. 💎 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/XXl/leverage-at-all-time-highs-ami*

### Post #1689: 2025-09-12 22:15 `[Discuss]`

**How much bitcoin is mine a day?**

Mined… not mine😬

Bitcoin mining output (post-halving, April 2024): about 450 BTC/day are mined.

Institutional demand alone: recent data shows ~1,755 BTC/day net accumulation from companies, ETFs, and other institutions. 

https://cryptonews.com/news/businesses-buy-1755-bitcoin-daily-adding-1-3-trillion-in-20-months-btc-above-125k-next/

That’s about 4× more than what miners are producing.

In 2028, only 225 bitcoin will be mined a day.. 🤔

FYI, in 2020, more bitcoins were mined VS what institutions were buying as it was not widely adopted..￼ 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/ZXS/how-much-bitcoin-is-mine-a-day*

### Post #1717: 2025-10-16 23:10 — `$SNAP` `[Dd]`

**$SNAP**

Wall Street shows little affection for $SNAP—it’s a stock loathed by many investors and a company scorned by others. Its valuation has essentially been slashed in half (in my view). Given the current bullish market vibe, it ought to be trading at $16 at minimum… Ah, but that’s the unpredictable nature of the markets for you
.
Fun fact: Over the past 4-5 years, $SNAP has poured roughly $7.8 billion into R&D, with a heavy focus on AI and AR innovations. (They could practically build a second Snapchat from scratch at this point…talk about commitment! 😅)

The user base remains steady and isn’t contracting in fact, it’s grown to 469 million daily active users globally as of Q2 2025, up 2% from the prior quarter. 

And let’s be real, it’s geared toward the younger crowd, not “vintage” folks 👴🏻, which only amps up its long-term appeal in a youth-driven digital economy.

Sure, there are hotter sectors out there like energy or China plays, but $SNAP strikes me as an overlooked gem. 

How much longer will it stay suppressed under $8.

And what shakes out with TikTok? The latest U.S. enforcement delay has been pushed to December 16, 2025, buying more time for a potential divestiture deal amid ongoing U.S.-China talks. 

If TikTok gets fully sidelined or sold off, that could funnel users (and ad dollars) straight to Snapchat..major tailwind potential.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/WarrenBuffett/58l/dollarsnap*

Adobe OWNS the file formats the entire world runs on:

PDF
PSD
Premiere projects
Illustrator vectors￼

Hollywood, agencies, and every pro creator are locked in for life. Switching cost? Retraining millions + breaking trillion-dollar pipelines.￼

Meanwhile Firefly is:
✅ Trained 100% legally (no lawsuits incoming)
✅ Built straight into the apps pros already use
✅ Has Content Credentials so brands can actually use the output

Numbers don’t lie:
+11% revenue
+12% ARR
45% FCF margins
$20B+ buybacks coming
Trading <30× forward earnings


The market is pricing Adobe like it’s dying.
